# Steal a Brainrot (SAB) fact sheet for the MASSAB riddle mode.
# This is the Roblox game, not Fortnite's "Steal the Brainrot" map. Promo codes appear only in "Codes and promotions", each with a dated status, and no code there is claimed to work today.
# Researched on 2026-11-04 and 2026-11-05, and expanded on 2026-11-06. "As of November 2026" means the value was read from the cited page on those dates.
# Lines in "Upcoming and unreleased content" are teasers and leaks, not confirmed releases, and they go stale quickly.
# Lines about Sammy use the word Sammy as well as SpyderSammy, because the matcher treats them as different words.
# Income and cost numbers change when the developer buffs or nerfs a Brainrot, so older guides often show other numbers.
# Rule for fact lines: a value stays in a fact line only if the sources read agree on it, or one credible source states it and nothing contradicts it.
# Credible here means the Roblox API, Sportskeeda, Beebom, Dexerto, PocketGamer.biz, Story Kitchen, Wikipedia, and the Steal a Brainrot Fandom wiki (community run).
# If sources are split with no clear majority, the disputed value is left out of the fact line and every side is in Needs review.
# If a clear majority agrees against one stray or weak page, the fact keeps the majority value and the disagreement is still in Needs review.
# Weak pages are SEO or marketplace blogs such as rblxguide, u7buy, Eldorado, Playgama, Finding Dulcinea and The Game of Nerds.
# Sportskeeda's www pages block automated fetches, so their text was read from the hindi3.sportskeeda.com copy of the same article. The www URLs cited here were opened in a browser to confirm they load.
# Lines added on 2026-11-06 carry extra fields inside the source tag, which the matcher strips along with it: id, type (official, secondary or community), status (confirmed, corroborated or unverified), confidence, pub (when the source was published), event (when it happened), checked (when it was verified) and note.
# status: confirmed means a primary source or two independent reputable sources, corroborated means two independent sources, unverified means one source or community only. Fields with no known value are left out.
# Also treated as credible from 2026-11-06: Roblox's shareholder letter on sec.gov, playsab.com, Guinness World Records, GamesRadar, TechWiser, IGN, Know Your Meme, Insider Gaming and Pocket Tactics.
# Lines in "Brainrot stats" were generated by script from each page's wiki infobox, so they are only as current as the wiki.

## What each brainrot is

# Each line says what a Brainrot is and its rarity. Rarities and looks come from the Fandom wiki as read on 2026-11-05.
# The first lines repeat the best known Brainrots in Wikipedia's words, since those are the words a riddle answer is likely to use.

Tralalero Tralala is a three-legged shark wearing Nike sneakers. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot]
Tung Tung Tung Sahur is made of wood, a wooden log holding a wooden bat. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot]
Bombardiro Crocodilo has the head of a crocodile and the body of a World War II bomber plane. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot]
Ballerina Cappuccina is a ballerina with a cappuccino mug for a head. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot]
Lirilì Larilà, also written Lirili Larila, is a cactus elephant wearing sandals. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot]
Chimpanzini Bananini is a chimpanzee with a banana for a body. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot]
Trippi Troppi is a cat with a shrimp's body. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot]
Tralalero Tralala is a shark with three legs wearing blue shoes, and its rarity is Brainrot God. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Tralalero_Tralala]
Tung Tung Tung Sahur is a tall piece of wood with a smiling face, holding a bat, and its rarity is Secret. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Tung_Tung_Tung_Sahur]
Bombardiro Crocodilo is a crocodile with a black bomber plane for a body, and its rarity is Mythic. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bombardiro_Crocodilo]
Lirilì Larilà, also written Lirili Larila, is an elephant with a cactus body wearing brown sandals, and its rarity is Common. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Liril%C3%AC_Laril%C3%A0]
Ballerina Cappuccina is a ballerina with a cup for a head, wearing a pink tutu, and its rarity is Legendary. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Ballerina_Cappuccina]

Chimpanzini Bananini is a chimpanzee inside a half-peeled banana, and its rarity is Legendary. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Chimpanzini_Bananini]
Trippi Troppi is a cat and shrimp mix, with a shrimp body and a cat face, and its rarity is Rare. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Trippi_Troppi]
Boneca Ambalabu is a frog head on a car tire, standing on two human legs, and its rarity is Rare. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Boneca_Ambalabu]
Bombombini Gusini is a goose with an airplane for a body and wings, and its rarity is Mythic. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bombombini_Gusini]

Wombo Rollo is a wombat with a smug face, and its rarity is Epic. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Wombo_Rollo]
Yess my Examen is a happy exam paper with an A+ grade, and its rarity is Secret. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Yess_my_Examen]
Yess my Resume is a white resume paper with a happy face, a blue tie, and a hired stamp, and its rarity is Secret. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Yess_my_Resume]
Yeti Claus is a yeti in Santa's outfit riding a red snowmobile, and its rarity is Brainrot God. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Yeti_Claus]
Yetimatic is a yeti fused into an ATM, and its rarity is Secret. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Yetimatic]
Zebrino Pianino is a zebra fused with a grand piano, and its rarity is Secret. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Zebrino_Pianino]
Zibra Zubra Zibralini is a zebra head on a watermelon with four horse legs, and its rarity is Mythic. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Zibra_Zubra_Zibralini]
Zombie Bowl is a zombie fish inside a circular container, and its rarity is Secret. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Zombie_Bowl]
Zombie Tralala is a zombie version of Tralalero Tralala, and its rarity is Secret. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Zombie_Tralala]

## Game overview

Steal a Brainrot is a Roblox game published by the Roblox group BRAZILIAN SPYDER. [source: https://games.roblox.com/v1/games?universeIds=7709344486]
Steal a Brainrot launched on Roblox in May 2025. [source: https://www.story-kitchen.com/news/story-kitchen-partnering-with-steal-a-brainrot-creators-on-feature-adaptation-of-roblox-game]
The official Roblox record for Steal a Brainrot shows the experience was created on May 16, 2025 at 00:49 UTC. [source: https://games.roblox.com/v1/games?universeIds=7709344486]
Steal a Brainrot servers hold a maximum of 8 players. [source: https://games.roblox.com/v1/games?universeIds=7709344486]
Roblox files Steal a Brainrot under the Simulation genre and the Tycoon subgenre. [source: https://games.roblox.com/v1/games?universeIds=7709344486]
The official description of Steal a Brainrot tells players to buy a Brainrot, steal Brainrots from other players, generate money, rebirth, and buy slaps and troll gear. [source: https://games.roblox.com/v1/games?universeIds=7709344486]
The official description of Steal a Brainrot credits Chris for letting the game use the Nyan Cat and WinnersTakesAll for the backpack system. [source: https://games.roblox.com/v1/games?universeIds=7709344486]
The Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot are characters based on the 2025 Italian brainrot memes. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot]
The gameplay loop of Steal a Brainrot has been compared to capture the flag. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot]
Steal a Brainrot gets a new update every Saturday, usually at 3 PM EST, and an Admin Abuse of about 30 to 60 minutes follows it. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse]
The latest Steal a Brainrot update as of early November 2026 is Update 73 on October 31, 2026, with Update 74 listed for November 7, 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log]
The Steal a Brainrot Fandom wiki counts 557 indexable Brainrots as of November 2026, or 571 with Lucky Blocks and other miscellaneous entries. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Brainrots]
Steal a Brainrot's Roblox record had 15,504,929 upvotes and 2,708,823 downvotes as of November 6, 2026. [source: https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/votes?universeIds=7709344486 | id=A02-0005 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Vote counts from the Roblox votes API, a snapshot taken on November 6, 2026.]
As of November 6, 2026, Steal a Brainrot sells three Roblox game passes: Admin Commands for 7,999 Robux, VIP for 375 Robux, and 2x Money for 299 Robux. [source: https://apis.roblox.com/game-passes/v1/universes/7709344486/game-passes?passView=Full&pageSize=100 | id=A02-0006 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=All three passes are listed for sale in the Roblox game-pass API, with no further pages. Prices can change.]

## Machines

The fuse-style machines in Steal a Brainrot released in this order: Fuse Machine (August 2025), Witch Fuse (October 2025), Santa's Fuse (December 2025), Divine Fuse (March 2026), Summer Fuse (May 2026), Haunted Fuse (October 2026). [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log]
The Rainbow Machine was the first machine in Steal a Brainrot, added in June 2025 (June 21, 2025); players deposited 25 Gold Brainrots to start a 15-minute Rainbow event. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rainbow_Machine]
The Bubblegum Machine in Steal a Brainrot was added in July 2025 (July 18, 2025), needed 10 Candy Brainrots, and was the first trait machine. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bubblegum_Machine]
The first Fuse Machine in Steal a Brainrot released in August 2025 (August 1, 2025). [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-fuse-machine-update-patch-notes]
The first Fuse Machine in Steal a Brainrot followed the Bubblegum Machine and stood next to the Coin Shop. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Fuse_Machine]
The Fuse Machine in Steal a Brainrot takes 4 Brainrots plus cash, and the cost and wait depend on the rarity of the Brainrots submitted. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-fuse-machine-update-patch-notes]
At launch the Steal a Brainrot Fuse Machine offered 12 Brainrots including 2 Secrets, Agarrini la Palini and Los Combinasionas. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_10]
The Steal a Brainrot Fuse Machine waits 15 minutes when the rarest input is Common, Rare or Epic, 30 minutes for Legendary, Mythic or Brainrot God, and 1 hour 30 minutes for Secret or OG. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Fuse_Machine]
Players could reveal a Steal a Brainrot Fuse Machine result early for 299 Robux. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-fuse-machine-guide]
The first Fuse Machine in Steal a Brainrot was removed in September 2025 (September 6, 2025) and replaced by the Craft Machine. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Fuse_Machine]
The Fuse Machine returned to Steal a Brainrot in January 2026 (January 24, 2026 at 3 PM EST) and the Divine Fuse later replaced it. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Fuse_Machine]
The Craft Machine in Steal a Brainrot released in September 2025 (September 6, 2025) with Update 15 and replaced the Fuse Machine. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_15]
Each Steal a Brainrot Craft Machine recipe uses 4 Brainrots, and the craftable options change every 30 minutes. [source: https://techwiser.com/steal-a-brainrot-craft-machine-how-use-guide/]
The Craft Machine in Steal a Brainrot was removed in October 2025 (October 11, 2025 at 3 PM EST) and replaced by the Witch Fuse. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Craft_Machine]
The Craft Machine returned to Steal a Brainrot in September 2026 (September 12, 2026) with Update 66. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_66]
The Witch Fuse in Steal a Brainrot released in October 2025 (October 11, 2025 at 3 PM EST) and the Brainrot Dealer replaced it in November 2025 (November 8, 2025). [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Witch_Fuse]
The Witch Fuse in Steal a Brainrot is a Halloween cauldron next to the Shop that accepts any 4 Brainrots and uses cash to start the fusion. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-witch-fuse-guide]
Headless Horseman was the highest-income Brainrot in the Steal a Brainrot Witch Fuse at $175M/s when Sportskeeda's guide was written in October 2025. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-witch-fuse-guide]
The Witch Fuse in Steal a Brainrot introduced the rule that mixed inputs give a Brainrot one rarity below the highest input, and it cut the chance of inheriting traits and mutations from 10-20% to 5-15%. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Witch_Fuse]
The Brainrot Dealer in Steal a Brainrot replaced the Witch Fuse in November 2025 (November 8, 2025), restocked Brainrots every 30 minutes, and was dropped after player complaints. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Brainrot_Dealer]
The Brainrot Trader in Steal a Brainrot replaced the Brainrot Dealer in November 2025 (November 15, 2025) and offered four trades every two hours. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Brainrot_Trader]
The Admin Machine in Steal a Brainrot was added in November 2025 (November 29, 2025) and removed in December 2025 (December 13, 2025). [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Machine]
Santa's Fuse in Steal a Brainrot released in December 2025 and replaced the Brainrot Trader in the lobby. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-santa-s-fuse-machine-guide]
Santa's Fuse in Steal a Brainrot takes four Brainrots and gives limited-time Christmas Brainrots, and a Brainrot made in it cannot be fused again. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-santa-s-fuse-machine-guide]
The Duels Machine in Steal a Brainrot lets two players wager one Brainrot each and fight in a private server, and the winner takes the other Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Duels_Machine]
The Trade Machine in Steal a Brainrot released in February 2026 and lets players send trade requests in their server and across servers. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_39]
The Divine Fuse in Steal a Brainrot released in March 2026 (March 7, 2026) with Update 41 and replaced the original Fuse Machine. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_41]
Sammy leaked the Steal a Brainrot Divine Fuse on February 28, 2026 during an Admin Abuse, and the community picked it in a poll over bringing back the OG Craft Machine. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Divine_Fuse]
The Divine Fuse in Steal a Brainrot was later replaced by the Cyber Craft Machine. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Divine_Fuse]
Griffin is the top Divine Fuse Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot, with an income of $400M/s. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Griffin]
The Cyber Craft Machine in Steal a Brainrot released in April 2026 (April 18, 2026) with Update 47 and launched with 11 Brainrots. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_47]
The Summer Fuse in Steal a Brainrot released in May 2026 (May 23, 2026) with Update 52 and holds 19 Brainrots. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Summer_Fuse]
Dragon Aquanini is the top Summer Fuse Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot, with an income of $375M/s. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Summer_Fuse]
The Summer Fuse in Steal a Brainrot was removed in August 2026 (August 1, 2026) with Update 60. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_60]
Los Traders in Steal a Brainrot released in July 2026 (July 11, 2026) and refreshes its trade offers every 30 minutes. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_57]
Los Traders was removed from Steal a Brainrot in August 2026 (August 8, 2026) when the RNG Machine arrived. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_61]
The RNG Machine in Steal a Brainrot released in August 2026 (August 8, 2026) as a luck-based machine with Legendary to Secret Brainrots and upgrades tied to rebirths. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/RNG_Machine]
The Taco Merchant in Steal a Brainrot appeared for one hour on the August 18, 2026 Taco Tuesday and sold Burrito Bat, Tacoturbo Tacorito, Nachorilla, and Sammyni Truckini for tacos. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Taco_Merchant]
The Headless Merchant in Steal a Brainrot ran from October 31 to November 1, 2026 and sold Brainrots for Candy Corns from the Trick or Treat event. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Headless_Merchant]
The Haunted Fuse in Steal a Brainrot released in October 2026 (October 3, 2026) with Update 69, replaced the Craft Machine, and looks like a haunted house. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Haunted_Fuse]

## Events and Admin Abuse

Admin Abuse in Steal a Brainrot is a special event where a host such as Sammy, Steak, or Caylus uses admin powers to spawn rare Brainrots, raise luck, and run server-wide events. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse]
Sammy often gives an early bird present, usually a Secret Lucky Block, to Steal a Brainrot players who are online about one hour before an Admin Abuse. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse]
The Bloodmoon Event in Steal a Brainrot lasted 15 minutes, spawned every 3 hours, and is listed by the Fandom wiki as the first event in the game. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_02]
Taco Tuesday in Steal a Brainrot is a weekly Admin Abuse event hosted by the developer every Tuesday. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/when-is-taco-tuesday-steal-a-brainrot]
The Taco event in Steal a Brainrot is one of the game's earliest events and gives random Brainrots the Taco trait from a cannon above the conveyor belt entrance. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-taco-event-guide]
The Taco trait in Steal a Brainrot gives a 3x income multiplier and puts a taco on the Brainrot's head. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-taco-event-guide]
The Raining Tacos song plays when the Steal a Brainrot Taco Tuesday event opens. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/when-is-taco-tuesday-steal-a-brainrot]
Taco Lucky Blocks and Los Taco Blocks spawn on the conveyor belt during Steal a Brainrot Taco Tuesday, and a Los Taco Block costs $300,000,000. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/when-is-taco-tuesday-steal-a-brainrot]
Fat Sammy in Steal a Brainrot is a Taco Tuesday NPC next to the Robux Shop who takes 10 Brainrots with the Taco trait, down from 15 at launch. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Taco_Tuesday]
Fat Sammy's reward odds in Steal a Brainrot are Tipi Topi Taco 75%, Capi Taco 12.5%, Bombardini Tortinii 10%, and Nooo My Hotspot 2.5%. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Fat_Sammy]
Tacorita Bicicleta was added on the September 16, 2025 Taco Tuesday in Steal a Brainrot as a limited Brainrot with a stock of one million. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Taco_Tuesday]
The Extinct Event in Steal a Brainrot spawned 1 of 3 limited Brainrots on the Red Carpet every 2 hours. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_16]
The Witching Hour event in Steal a Brainrot ran every two hours and spawned Vampira Cappucina, Zombie Tralala, Frankentteo, or La Vacca Jacko Linterino on the conveyor belt. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-witch-fuse-guide]
The Winter Hour event in Steal a Brainrot ran every two hours for 3 to 4 minutes and gave Brainrots the Santa Hat trait. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_28]
The Turkey Event in Steal a Brainrot ran around Thanksgiving 2025 and spawned Gobblino Uniciclino on the map, which players had to hit 10 times. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Turkey_Event]
The Bruno Mars concert in Steal a Brainrot took place on January 17, 2026, lasted just under eight minutes, was pre-recorded, and featured five identical Bruno Mars avatars. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/steal-a-brainrots-bruno-mars-concert-hits-12-7-million-concurrent-players-3305913/]
The Escape Tsunami limited time mode in Steal a Brainrot was added on January 24, 2026 and removed on April 4, 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Escape_Tsunami_LTM]
Stealing was disabled in the Steal a Brainrot Escape Tsunami limited time mode. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_35]
The Backrooms Event in Steal a Brainrot runs every 2 hours on weekdays and every hour on weekends, and Evil Sammy entities guard it. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Backrooms_Event]
The Jump for Eggs limited time mode in Steal a Brainrot has players jump between islands to collect eggs and earn XP on trampolines to unlock new islands. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Jump_for_Eggs_LTM]
The Event Board that ran the Halloween event traits in Steal a Brainrot was removed on November 2, 2025 at 11:00 AM EST. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Events | id=A05-0014 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-11-02 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Events page does not name the board in this row; the Halloween page gives the same removal time for the Halloween traits.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Halloween | checked=2026-11-06]
The New Year's Machine in Steal a Brainrot was added on December 31, 2025 at 12 PM EST. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/New_Year%27s_Machine | id=A05-0022 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-12-31 | checked=2026-11-06]
The Easter Event Board in Steal a Brainrot was added on April 4, 2026 at 3 PM EST with Easter Part 2. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Easter_Eventboard | id=A05-0032 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-04-04 | checked=2026-11-06]
The John Pork event in Steal a Brainrot was released on May 2, 2026 at 3 PM EST. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/John_Pork_Event | id=A05-0039 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-05-02 | checked=2026-11-06]
The Spain Event in Steal a Brainrot started on July 25, 2026 and added the Spain trait. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Spain_Event | id=A05-0051 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-07-25 | checked=2026-11-06]
Fortunu and Cashuru is the rarest Brainrot obtainable from the Leprechaun Lucky Block in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Fortunu_and_Cashuru | id=A05-0085 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-03-14 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Release date is given as 14/3/2026 on the same page.]

## Rebirths

Rebirth in Steal a Brainrot takes the required Brainrots and most of your money, and gives new gear, a higher multiplier, and usually more base slots. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth]
Steal a Brainrot has 19 rebirths as of November 2026, and each one adds 10 seconds to the Lock Base time. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 1 is the only Steal a Brainrot rebirth that does not add a base slot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth]
Before Update 66 a Steal a Brainrot rebirth took every Brainrot in your base; since then it takes only the required Brainrots. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth]
Rebirth 1 in Steal a Brainrot costs $500K and requires Trippi Troppi and Gangster Footera; it unlocks the Friend Controller, Iron Slap, Gravity Coil, and Bee Launcher. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 1 in Steal a Brainrot used to require Tung Tung Tung Sahur until Gangster Footera replaced it after the character's removal. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth]
Rebirth 2 in Steal a Brainrot costs $1.5M and requires Brr Brr Patapim and Boneca Ambalabu; it unlocks Gold Slap, Coil Combo, Rage Table, and the second base floor. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth]
Rebirth 3 in Steal a Brainrot costs $12.5M and requires Trulimero Trulicina and Chimpanzini Bananini; it unlocks Diamond Slap, Grapple Hook, and Taser Gun. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 4 in Steal a Brainrot costs $35M and requires Chef Crabracadabra and Glorbo Fruttodrillo; it unlocks Emerald Slap, Invisibility Cloak, and Boogie Bomb. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 5 in Steal a Brainrot costs $100M and requires Frigo Camelo and Orangutini Ananassini; it unlocks Ruby Slap and Medusa's Head. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 6 in Steal a Brainrot costs $350M and requires Bombardiro Crocodilo; it unlocks Dark Matter Slap and Web Slinger. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 7 in Steal a Brainrot costs $1B and requires Bombombini Gusini; it unlocks Flame Slap, Quantum Cloner, and All Seeing Sentry. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 8 in Steal a Brainrot costs $5B and requires Te Te Te Sahur; it unlocks Nuclear Slap and Rainbowrath Sword. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 9 in Steal a Brainrot costs $12.5B and requires Cocofanto Elefanto; it unlocks Galaxy Slap and Laser Cape. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 10 in Steal a Brainrot costs $125B and requires Girafa Celestre; it unlocks Glitched Slap, Body Swap Potion, and the third base floor. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth]
Rebirth 11 in Steal a Brainrot costs $800B and requires Tralalero Tralala; it unlocks Splatter Slap and Paintball Gun. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 12 in Steal a Brainrot costs $3.5T and requires Odin Din Din Dun; it unlocks Heart Balloon and Magnet. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 13 in Steal a Brainrot requires Trenostruzzo Turbo 3000 and unlocks Megaphone and BeeHive. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 14 in Steal a Brainrot costs $40T and requires Trippi Troppi Troppa Trippa; it unlocks Gummy Bear and Subspace Mine. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 15 in Steal a Brainrot costs $100T and requires Pakrahmatmamat; it unlocks Heatseeker. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 16 in Steal a Brainrot requires the Secret Brainrot Los Tralaleritos and unlocks Attack Doge, and it is the first rebirth to require a Secret. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth]
Rebirth 17 in Steal a Brainrot requires Job Job Job Sahur and Chicleteira Bicicleteira and unlocks Giant Potion. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 18 in Steal a Brainrot costs $10Qa and requires Graipuss Medussi; it unlocks Flash Teleport. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 19 in Steal a Brainrot costs $30Qa and requires La Grande Combinasion. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/]
Rebirth 19 in Steal a Brainrot raised the base from 27 to 28 slots. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_62]
Rebirth 16 in Steal a Brainrot requires 1Qa ($1 quadrillion) in cash and Los Tralaleritos in the base. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-rebirth-16-update-guide | id=A04-0001 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | event=2025-10-04 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Beebom lists $10Qa for Rebirth 16, which conflicts with its own Rebirth 18 figure; the Fandom wiki and Eldorado both give $1Qa.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://www.eldorado.gg/blog/steal-a-brainrot-en/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide/ | checked=2026-11-06]
Rebirth 13 in Steal a Brainrot costs $14T and requires Trenostruzzo Turbo 3000. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth | id=A04-0002 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Beebom says $14T or $35T; Sportskeeda's July 2025 notes said $15T before the Rebirth 14 reshuffle.] [source: https://www.eldorado.gg/blog/steal-a-brainrot-en/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide/ | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/ | checked=2026-11-06]
Rebirth 17 in Steal a Brainrot costs $2Qa and requires Job Job Job Sahur and Chicleteira Bicicleteira. [source: https://www.eldorado.gg/blog/steal-a-brainrot-en/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide/ | id=A04-0003 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | pub=2026-08-16 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Beebom lists $2.50Qa for Rebirth 17; the Fandom wiki agrees with $2Qa.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth | checked=2026-11-06]
Rebirth 19 in Steal a Brainrot gives a 19x cash multiplier and a one-time payout of 250 trillion cash. [source: https://allthings.how/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-19-guide-requirements-and-rewards/ | id=A04-0004 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The payout is a one-time cash refund after the reset, not an ongoing income boost.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://www.eldorado.gg/blog/steal-a-brainrot-en/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide/ | checked=2026-11-06]
Steal a Brainrot's rebirth multipliers skip x14: Rebirth 14 gives x13 and Rebirth 15 gives x15. [source: https://www.eldorado.gg/blog/steal-a-brainrot-en/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide/ | id=A04-0006 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | pub=2026-08-16 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Fandom wiki gives the same multiplier steps. Beebom instead lists x14 at Rebirth 14 and x15 at Rebirth 15.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth | checked=2026-11-06]
In Steal a Brainrot, if a Brainrot is being stolen and the owner rebirths, the Brainrot disappears. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/ | id=A04-0008 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth | checked=2026-11-06]
In Steal a Brainrot, Brainrots still walking to the base when the owner rebirths go back to the Red Carpet. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rebirth | id=A04-0009 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-guide-levels-and-rewards/ | checked=2026-11-06]

## Bases, stealing and trading

The base in Steal a Brainrot stores your Brainrots, which generate cash over time, and it can have up to 3 floors. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Base]
The first floor of a Steal a Brainrot base has 10 slots, Rebirth 2 unlocks the second floor, and Rebirth 10 unlocks the third floor. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Base]
The Lock Base in Steal a Brainrot starts at 60 seconds, gains 10 seconds per rebirth, and gains a fixed 10 seconds with the VIP gamepass. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Base]
The Red Carpet in Steal a Brainrot, also called the Conveyor Belt, is where Brainrots walk across the map to be bought. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Red_Carpet]
A Brainrot bought from the Steal a Brainrot Red Carpet can be bought by another player while it walks to the base for 150% of its base cost. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Red_Carpet]
When a Brainrot is being stolen in Steal a Brainrot, the base owner gets a message and an alarm sound. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Stealing]
A player carrying a stolen Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot cannot use gears, and their speed drops from 34 to 20 and their jump height from 7.2 to 4.32. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Stealing]
The Steal a Brainrot steal message uses the rarity color: green Common, blue Rare, purple Epic, yellow Legendary, red Mythic, rainbow Brainrot God, black and white Secret, black and yellow OG. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Stealing]
The Steal a Brainrot Index gives a +0.5x base multiplier for completing the Default, Gold, Diamond, or Rainbow Index, which each need 75% of the game's Brainrots. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Index]
The Trade Plaza in Steal a Brainrot is a place where stealing is disabled and has three sections: the normal plaza, the Pro plaza (25B/s), and the OG plaza (100B/s and an OG Brainrot). [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Trading_Plaza]
Lucky Blocks in Steal a Brainrot can be bought and stolen like Brainrots, do not generate money, and open into a random Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Lucky_Blocks]
The Secret Lucky Block in Steal a Brainrot costs $750M. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Secret_Lucky_Block]
Los Lucky Blocks in Steal a Brainrot are exclusive to Admin Abuse and cost $250,000,000 on the conveyor. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-rebirth-16-update-guide]
Offline cash in Steal a Brainrot stops accumulating 5 hours after the player's last in-game session. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Offline_Cash | id=A04-0011 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Only the Fandom wiki states this cap; no independent source was found. The offline rate is disputed (see the offline cash rate conflict).]
In Steal a Brainrot, the Like Goal Milestone spawned a random Brainrot God each time the game reached another 100,000 likes, and it could not run in private servers. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Like_Goal_Milestone | id=A04-0014 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Fandom describes the milestone in the past tense, and it may no longer run. The corroborating Allthings guide (August 2026) mentions Brainrot God spawns at like milestones.] [source: https://allthings.how/steal-a-brainrot-roblox-efficient-stealing-events-and-rebirths | checked=2026-11-06]
In Steal a Brainrot, Brainrot God and Secret spawns on the Red Carpet are announced only in the player's own server, while OG spawns are also shown in the Live Spawns panel. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Red_Carpet | id=A04-0015 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]

## Mutations and traits

A Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot can have only one Mutation but any number of Traits. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-all-traits-multipliers]
The Steal a Brainrot Mutation multipliers include Gold 1.25x, Diamond 1.5x, Bloodrot 2x, Candy 4x, Lava 6x, Yin Yang 7.5x, Radioactive 8.5x, Cursed 9x, Rainbow 10x, Divine 10x, Cyber 11x, Phantom 12x, and Crystal 13x. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
Gold and Diamond were the first mutations in Steal a Brainrot, added in May 2025 (May 31, 2025). [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
The Gold mutation in Steal a Brainrot has a 10% chance on the Red Carpet, Diamond has 5%, and Rainbow has 1%. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
The Rainbow mutation in Steal a Brainrot was added in June 2025 (June 21, 2025) and gives a 10x multiplier. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
The Bloodrot mutation in Steal a Brainrot gives a 2x multiplier and comes from the Bloodmoon event. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/all-mutations-effects-steal-brainrot]
The Candy mutation in Steal a Brainrot was added in July 2025 (July 5, 2025) and gives a 4x multiplier during the Candy Aurora event. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
The Lava mutation in Steal a Brainrot was added in July 2025 (July 25, 2025) and gives a 6x multiplier during the Molten event. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
The Galaxy mutation in Steal a Brainrot was added in August 2025 (August 23, 2025) and comes from the Galactic event. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
The Yin Yang mutation in Steal a Brainrot was added in September 2025 (September 27, 2025) and gives a 7.5x multiplier. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
The Radioactive mutation in Steal a Brainrot was added in November 2025 (November 15, 2025) and gives an 8.5x multiplier. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
The Cursed mutation in Steal a Brainrot was added in January 2026 (January 3, 2026) and gives a 9x multiplier. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
The Divine mutation in Steal a Brainrot was added in February 2026 (February 21, 2026), gives a 10x multiplier, and can also give the Halo trait. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
The Cyber mutation in Steal a Brainrot was added in April 2026 (April 18, 2026) and gives an 11x multiplier. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
The Phantom mutation in Steal a Brainrot was added in June 2026 (June 13, 2026) and gives a 12x multiplier. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
The Crystal mutation in Steal a Brainrot was added in July 2026 (July 25, 2026) and gives a 13x multiplier. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
The Eclipse mutation in Steal a Brainrot was added in October 2026, is listed as the best mutation, and was called Celestial before release. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Eclipse_Event]
The Celestial mutation in Steal a Brainrot gave a 4x multiplier and could only be activated by the developer as of June 2026. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/all-mutations-effects-steal-brainrot]
Collecting 75% of the Brainrots in one mutation unlocks that mutation's base skin in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
Beebom and the Fandom wiki both treat Steal a Brainrot mutation and trait multipliers as additive, so a Lava mutation (6x) with a Bombardiro trait (4x) gives 10x. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-mutations-and-traits-list]
Traits in Steal a Brainrot can come from developer events, player rituals, or weather, and a Brainrot can hold any number of them. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-all-traits-multipliers]
The Taco trait in Steal a Brainrot gives 3x, the Concert trait 5x, the Crab trait 5x, the Paint trait 6x, the Nyan trait 6x, the Meowl trait 7x, and the Strawberry trait 8x. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-all-traits-multipliers]
The Strawberry trait in Steal a Brainrot only appears when a Strawberry Elephant spawns on a server. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-all-traits-multipliers]
The Sleepy trait is the only Steal a Brainrot trait that lowers income. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-all-traits-multipliers]
The Galaxy mutation in Steal a Brainrot gives a 7x multiplier as of November 2026. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/all-mutations-effects-steal-brainrot | id=A10-0001 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | pub=2026-06-29 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Beebom's November 2025 list gave Galaxy 6x, or 7x while the Galaxy event runs (older value). The Fandom Mutations page and Update 13 page also say 7x.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_13 | checked=2026-11-06]

## Rarities and common Brainrots

Steal a Brainrot has eight rarities, from lowest to highest: Common, Rare, Epic, Legendary, Mythic, Brainrot God, Secret, and OG. [source: https://beebom.com/all-brainrots-in-steal-a-brainrot/amp/]
The Secret rarity was added to Steal a Brainrot in May 2025 (May 31, 2025) with Update 1. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_01]
The OG rarity was added to Steal a Brainrot in August 2025 (August 30, 2025) with Update 14 and is rarer than Secret. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_14]
OG is the rarest class in Steal a Brainrot, and every OG except Headless Horseman has its own event when it spawns. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/OG]
Secret is the second-best rarity in Steal a Brainrot, with 270 Brainrots as of November 2026 and a spawn chance below 0.001%. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Secret]
The name of a Brainrot God is said out loud when it spawns in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Brainrot_God]
Noobini Pizzanini is a Common Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot that costs $25 and makes $1/s. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Noobini_Pizzanini]
The Common Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot include Noobini Pizzanini, Lirili Larila, Tim Cheese, FluriFlura, Talpa Di Fero, Svinina Bombardino, Pipi Kiwi, and Pipi Corni. [source: https://beebom.com/all-brainrots-in-steal-a-brainrot/amp/]
The Rare Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot include Trippi Troppi, Gangster Footera, Bandito Bobritto, Boneca Ambalabu, Cacto Hipopotamo, Ta Ta Ta Ta Sahur, and Tric Trac Baraboom. [source: https://beebom.com/all-brainrots-in-steal-a-brainrot/amp/]
The Epic Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot include Cappuccino Assassino, Brr Brr Patapim, Trulimero Trulicina, Bambini Crostini, Bananita Dolphinita, and Perochello Lemonchello. [source: https://beebom.com/all-brainrots-in-steal-a-brainrot/amp/]
The Legendary Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot include Burbaloni Loliloli, Ballerina Cappuccina, Chef Crabracadabra, Lionel Cactuseli, Glorbo Fruttodrillo, and Sigma Boy. [source: https://beebom.com/all-brainrots-in-steal-a-brainrot/amp/]
The Mythic Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot include Frigo Camelo, Orangutini Ananassini, Rhino Toasterino, Bombardiro Crocodilo, Bombombini Gusini, and Te Te Te Sahur. [source: https://beebom.com/all-brainrots-in-steal-a-brainrot/amp/]
The Brainrot God Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot include Girafa Celestre, Matteo, Tralalero Tralala, Odin Din Din Dun, Trenostruzzo Turbo 3000, and Trippi Troppi Troppa Trippa. [source: https://beebom.com/all-brainrots-in-steal-a-brainrot/amp/]

## OG Brainrots

The OG Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot as of November 2026 are Skibidi Toilet, John Pork, Headless Horseman, Meowl, Strawberry Elephant, and Spyder Elephant. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/OG]
Strawberry Elephant is the first OG Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot, released in August 2025 (August 30, 2025) with Update 14. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Strawberry_Elephant]
Strawberry Elephant in Steal a Brainrot costs $750B and has an income of $750M/s as of November 2026, up from $250M/s when it released. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Strawberry_Elephant]
Strawberry Elephant was first made in 2007 by a user named mzic2403 for a Photoshop competition, according to the Steal a Brainrot Fandom wiki. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Strawberry_Elephant]
Meowl is the second OG Brainrot added to Steal a Brainrot, released in October 2025 (October 18, 2025) with Update 21. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Meowl]
Meowl in Steal a Brainrot has an income of $600M/s and costs $650B as of November 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Meowl]
Skibidi Toilet is the third OG Brainrot added to Steal a Brainrot, released in December 2025 (December 27, 2025) with Update 31. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_31]
Skibidi Toilet in Steal a Brainrot has an income of $450M/s and costs $450B as of November 2026, the lowest income of any OG. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Skibidi_Toilet]
Headless Horseman was added to Steal a Brainrot in October 2025 (October 25, 2025) as a Witch Fuse Secret and became an OG in Update 40 in February 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Headless_Horseman]
John Pork is an OG Brainrot added to Steal a Brainrot in May 2026 (May 2, 2026) with Update 49, costing $500B with an income of $500M/s. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/John_Pork]
Spyder Elephant is an OG Brainrot released in Steal a Brainrot in May 2026 with Update 51, with an income of $1B/s and a cost of $1T. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Spyder_Elephant]
