## Rituals

Rituals in Steal a Brainrot are secret easter eggs activated by using specific Brainrots in a specific pattern, and the Brainrots used are permanently deleted. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rituals]
The La Vacca ritual in Steal a Brainrot uses 3 La Vacca Saturno Saturnita held in a triangle and spawns Las Vaquitas Saturnitas. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rituals]
The Spiderini ritual in Steal a Brainrot uses four Sammyni Spyderini in a square and gives Los Spyderinis. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_14]
The Chicleteira Bicicleteira ritual in Steal a Brainrot uses 2 Chicleteira Bicicleteira on the two sides of the Red Carpet and spawns Los Chicleteiras. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_16]
The Karkerkar ritual in Steal a Brainrot needs four players each holding Karkerkar Kurkur in a diamond shape, and it lets you buy Los Karkeritos for $200,000,000. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-rebirth-16-update-guide]
The 1x1x1x1 ritual in Steal a Brainrot uses 4 Guerriro Digitale in a diamond shape in the tube behind the Brainrot Spawner and gives 1x1x1x1 at 99.5% or Guest 666 at 0.5%. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_23]
The Job Job Job Sahur ritual in Steal a Brainrot uses four Job Job Job Sahur lined up in the Shop and gives Yess my Resume at 99% or Noo my Resume at 1%. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_60]
The Money Money Puggy ritual in Steal a Brainrot uses 2 Money Money Puggy to get Los Puggies. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_65]

## Developer, studio and legal

SpyderSammy, display name Sammy, owns the Roblox group BRAZILIAN SPYDER that publishes Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://groups.roblox.com/v1/groups/35815907]
The BRAZILIAN SPYDER Roblox group behind Steal a Brainrot had about 39.5 million members on November 4, 2026. [source: https://groups.roblox.com/v1/groups/35815907]
Wikipedia credits Steal a Brainrot to Sammy (SpyderSammy) and says DoBig Studios owns the game. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot]
Sammy (SpyderSammy), the Steal a Brainrot developer, is a chief officer at Do Big Studios. [source: https://roblox.fandom.com/wiki/Player:SpyderSammy]
Sammy (SpyderSammy), the Steal a Brainrot developer, started in game development in 2013 and also worked on Blade Ball, Race Clicker, Schedule, and Fisch. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy]
A Steal a Brainrot movie was announced in January 2026 (January 29, 2026), with Story Kitchen developing and producing it with Do Big Studios and Spyder Games. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-is-being-adapted-into-a-movie-3311810/]
The Steal a Brainrot movie was in early development with no release date when it was announced. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-is-being-adapted-into-a-movie-3311810/]
The Steal a Brainrot movie is led by Dmitri M. Johnson, Michael Lawrence Goldberg, Timothy I. Stevenson, and Elena Sandoval, with Think Influence producing in association. [source: https://www.story-kitchen.com/news/story-kitchen-partnering-with-steal-a-brainrot-creators-on-feature-adaptation-of-roblox-game]
Tung Tung Tung Sahur was removed from Steal a Brainrot in September 2025 amid licensing talks over the character. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/gaming/steal-a-brainrot-mascot-disappears-from-roblox-after-copyright-dispute-3252146/]
Tung Tung Tung Sahur returned to Steal a Brainrot in November 2025 (November 29, 2025) and was removed again in April 2026 (Update 48). [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_48]
In October 2025 Spyder Games and Speedy Simulator Gaming sued the creator of the Fortnite Creative map Stealing Brainrots, an unlicensed copy of Steal a Brainrot, for alleged copyright infringement. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot] [source: https://insider-gaming.com/steal-a-brainrot-developers-sue-fortnite-copycat/ | id=A15-0017 | type=secondary | checked=2026-11-06]
Epic Games licensed Steal a Brainrot for Fortnite Creative as Steal the Brainrot, released in July 2025, which is a separate game from the Roblox original. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot]
Steal a Brainrot won Best Creative Direction at the 2025 Roblox Innovation Awards, presented at RDC 2025. [source: https://beebom.com/roblox-innovation-awards-2025-winners]
The Star reported in March 2026 that Steal a Brainrot is made by Spyder Games, based in Louisiana, and Speedy Simulator Gaming, based in Wyoming. [source: https://www.thestar.com.my/tech/tech-news/2026/03/25/robloxs-hit-game-steal-a-brainrot-battles-its-many-imitators]
Spyder Games and Speedy Simulator Gaming, the companies behind Steal a Brainrot, had filed at least four lawsuits against alleged imitators by March 2026. [source: https://www.thestar.com.my/tech/tech-news/2026/03/25/robloxs-hit-game-steal-a-brainrot-battles-its-many-imitators]
Steal a Brainrot had earned an estimated US$64 million from in-game purchases by March 2026, according to Romonitor data cited by The Star. [source: https://www.thestar.com.my/tech/tech-news/2026/03/25/robloxs-hit-game-steal-a-brainrot-battles-its-many-imitators]
Adam Starr, general counsel at Do Big Studios, told GamesBeat in February 2026 that the company was trying to bring Steal a Brainrot to the big screen. [source: https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-creators-view-film-and-tv-adaptations-as-a-promising-revenue-stream/]
Mementum Lab SAS, a French company, sent a cease-and-desist over Tung Tung Sahur to Steal a Brainrot in September 2025. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/tung-tung-sahur-is-at-the-center-of-a-bizarre-federal-custody-battle-over-brainrot-characters-3381733/]
The companies behind Steal a Brainrot sued Mementum Lab in the US District Court for the Northern District of California, arguing that Tung Tung Sahur was AI-generated and has no valid copyright. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/tung-tung-sahur-is-at-the-center-of-a-bizarre-federal-custody-battle-over-brainrot-characters-3381733/]
A hearing in the Steal a Brainrot case against Mementum Lab was set for September 16, 2026, no judge had ruled on the core questions as of Dexerto's July 2026 report, and searches on November 6, 2026 found no report of a ruling. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/tung-tung-sahur-is-at-the-center-of-a-bizarre-federal-custody-battle-over-brainrot-characters-3381733/]
Steal a Brainrot has also been released on Meta Horizon for VR. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot_(Game)]
The BRAZILIAN SPYDER group behind Steal a Brainrot was created on April 1, 2025. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/BRAZILIAN_SPYDER]
do_small is a Brazilian Roblox developer, the co-creator of Steal a Brainrot, and a former Chief Operations Officer of Do Big Studios. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Do_Small]
The Fandom wiki lists do_big as the CEO of Do Big Studios, the company connected to Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Do_Small]
The Steal a Brainrot management team on the Fandom wiki lists SpyderSammy, do_small as co-owner, Atavi as QA Lead, and Guga as Game Producer. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/BRAZILIAN_SPYDER]
The official Steal a Brainrot accounts are StealaBrainrotR on X and YouTube and stealabrainrotdevs on TikTok. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/BRAZILIAN_SPYDER]
Do Big Studios' official website describes its offer to game developers as full acquisitions, minority investments, and operating partnerships. [source: https://www.dobigstudios.com/ | id=A01-0004 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The official site does not mention Steal a Brainrot on the pages read, so the game is not listed as a Do Big title there.]

## Sammy, the owner

Sammy, whose Roblox username is SpyderSammy, created Steal a Brainrot with do_small under the group BRAZILIAN SPYDER. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot_(Game)]
The Star's March 2026 report names Sammy (SpyderSammy), the Steal a Brainrot developer, as Sam Brakta. [source: https://www.thestar.com.my/tech/tech-news/2026/03/25/robloxs-hit-game-steal-a-brainrot-battles-its-many-imitators]
Sammy said Steal a Brainrot was inspired by Steal a Character, a game whose rights he acquired and improved. [source: https://www.thestar.com.my/tech/tech-news/2026/03/25/robloxs-hit-game-steal-a-brainrot-battles-its-many-imitators]
Sammy, the Steal a Brainrot owner, wrote on X: "I've been making games on ROBLOX since 2013." [source: https://www.eldorado.gg/blog/sammy-steal-a-brainrot-guide/]
Sammy's first original Roblox project before Steal a Brainrot was Crab Game, made in 2022 under the Spyder Games group. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy]
After Crab Game, Sammy made Mining Clicker Simulator under the Spyder Crew group, before Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy]
Sammy, the Steal a Brainrot owner, joined the Blade Ball team as a lead developer in late 2023. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy]
Sammy, the Steal a Brainrot owner, released Pixel Tower Defense in 2024 under the Red Spyder group, and it was discontinued in April 2025 after more than 26 million visits. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy]
Sammy, the Steal a Brainrot owner, released Line to Riches in early 2025 under the Blue Spyder group, a game for gathering donations with more than 15.6 million visits. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy]
The Roblox wiki also lists Sammy, the Steal a Brainrot owner, as a developer on Blue Lock: Rivals, DIG, Build A Plane, Steal a Car, TCG Card Shop Simulator, and Line to Fight. [source: https://roblox.fandom.com/wiki/Player:SpyderSammy]
Sammy, the Steal a Brainrot owner, posts as SpyderSammy on X, spydersammy on TikTok, and spydersammy333 on Instagram. [source: https://www.eldorado.gg/blog/sammy-steal-a-brainrot-guide/]
Sammy is most active on the official Steal a Brainrot Discord, especially the bombardiro-news channel. [source: https://www.eldorado.gg/blog/sammy-steal-a-brainrot-guide/]
The Fandom wiki lists the United States as the country of Sammy, the Steal a Brainrot owner. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy]
The Fandom wiki gives the birthday of Sammy, the Steal a Brainrot owner, as February 25. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy]
Sammy always says ":3" during Steal a Brainrot Admin Abuses. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy]
Sammy's Steal a Brainrot catchphrases include "I spoil y'all way too much", "someone said La Vacca?", "one more snap?", and "raining lucky block?". [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy]
Sammy's favorite Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot is Meowl, which he said in the community server's Exclusive Chat. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy]
Sammy's Roblox avatar is red themed, and red represented his team in the Steal a Brainrot Admin Abuse War. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy]
Jandel mocked Sammy as "Scammy Sammy" during the Steal a Brainrot Admin Abuse War, and detractors still use the nickname. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy]
Sammy usually mentions having tacos for dinner and often eats them during Steal a Brainrot Taco Tuesday. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse]
Sammy's Snap in Steal a Brainrot is an animation where Sammy runs down the Red Carpet with a red aura and snaps, which starts almost all in-game events at once. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse]
The Coffin Snap in Steal a Brainrot is Sammy's Snap with Sammy coming out of a coffin first. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse]
Raining Lucky Blocks is what Sammy calls spawning a large number of Lucky Blocks at once during a Steal a Brainrot Admin Abuse. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse]
Random Server Spawns let Sammy spawn a chosen Brainrot in a set number of Steal a Brainrot servers. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse]
Sammy runs Secret Admin Abuses in Steal a Brainrot mostly on Sundays and Friday nights, sometimes opening with "is anyone there". [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse]
Sammy's Base in Steal a Brainrot is an event first activated during the Admin Abuse War on August 23, 2025, with Brainrot God and Secret Brainrots inside. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Sammy%27s_Base]
Sammy's Base in Steal a Brainrot floats in the sky and has a small table with tacos and a chair, but it does not show Sammy's real base. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse]
Sammy spawned La Karkerkar Combinasion by mistake on the September 9, 2025 Steal a Brainrot Taco Tuesday and then nerfed it. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse]
Sammy added Raccooni Jandelini to Steal a Brainrot before the Admin Abuse War to poke fun at Jandel, the creator of Grow a Garden. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse]
Jandel, the owner of Grow a Garden, started the Admin Abuse War against Sammy and Steal a Brainrot on August 23, 2025, accusing Sammy of stealing ideas. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse_War]
Grow a Garden won the Admin Abuse War against Sammy's Steal a Brainrot on player count. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse]
During the Admin Abuse War, Sammy said Admin Abuse existed in Blox Fruits first, and Blox Fruits creator mygame43 appeared in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse_War]
Sammy took a 3-week break to work on the Steal a Brainrot Summer Update, and Steak became the first YouTuber to host an Admin Abuse. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Steak%27s_Admin_Abuse]
Steak's Steal a Brainrot Admin Abuse was meant to be hosted by Caylus, but Sammy let Steak host it instead. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Steak%27s_Admin_Abuse]
Sari, a YouTuber listed from Japan, hosted a Secret Admin Abuse in Steal a Brainrot for Japan and has a personal snap. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Sari]
The Steal a Brainrot Brainrots based on Sammy include Sammyni Spyderini, Sammyni Fattini, Sammyni Cakini, Sammyni Truckini, and Sammyni Spookyni. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Sammy_(Disambiguation)]
Spyder Elephant in Steal a Brainrot was created by Sammy, has Strawberry Elephant's look covered in diamonds, and comes from the Spyder Chain sold in Spyder Simulators. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Spyder_Elephant]
Spyder Elephant is the first unstealable Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot and the first OG obtainable with Robux. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Spyder_Elephant]
Sammy added polls to Steal a Brainrot on the February 17, 2026 Taco Tuesday, and the poll made Headless Horseman an OG. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Poll]
Sammy said on the April 7, 2026 Steal a Brainrot Taco Tuesday that adding an OG is slow because it needs permission from the meme's creator. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/OG]
Skibidi Toilet was teased for Steal a Brainrot on September 20, 2025 and released after a collaboration with Invisible Narratives, which owns the IP. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/OG]
A hacked developer spawned Rainbow Headless Horsemen in all Steal a Brainrot servers on October 22, 2026, and the game was shut down for about an hour. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot_(Game)]
Sammy said he would "make an example out of" the person behind the October 22, 2026 Steal a Brainrot hack. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot_(Game)]
A person using the alias John Doe breached Steal a Brainrot on March 18, 2026 and posted messages such as "W JANDEL" while Sammy was offline. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/John_Doe_Hacking]
SpyderSammy's official X profile, @SpyderSammy, describes Sammy as a game producer. [source: https://x.com/SpyderSammy | id=A01-0001 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Read on 2026-11-06 from the X profile page.]
SpyderSammy's X account, @SpyderSammy, was created in April 2021. [source: https://x.com/SpyderSammy | id=A01-0002 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | event=2021-04 | checked=2026-11-06]
Crab Game, SpyderSammy's first original Roblox game, was closed in August 2022 and has been unplayable since then, as of November 2026. [source: https://roblox.fandom.com/wiki/Player:SpyderSammy | id=A01-0005 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2022-08 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Only the Roblox wiki gives this; the Steal a Brainrot wiki says the game was made in 2022.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy | checked=2026-11-06]
SpyderSammy joined the Wiggity. development team as a lead developer for Blade Ball in late 2023. [source: https://roblox.fandom.com/wiki/Player:SpyderSammy | id=A01-0006 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2023-12 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Steal a Brainrot wiki says he joined the Blade Ball team in late 2023 without naming Wiggity. This adds the team name only from the Roblox wiki.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/SpyderSammy | checked=2026-11-06]

## Upcoming and unreleased content

Steal a Brainrot Update 74 is listed for November 7, 2026, and its content had not been announced as of November 5, 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_74]
Smurf Cat is an upcoming OG Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot with no release date as of November 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Smurf_Cat]
Sammy hinted at Smurf Cat for Steal a Brainrot at the September 27, 2025 Admin Abuse by saying "y'all DONT believe the OGs im planning" and sending mushroom and cat emojis. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Smurf_Cat]
Sammy also hinted at Smurf Cat for Steal a Brainrot by saying "hopefully the next base skin is a blue with a mushroom on top". [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Smurf_Cat]
Smurf Cat, the upcoming Steal a Brainrot OG, was created by artist Nate Hallinan in 2014 and may be held back by copyright. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Smurf_Cat]
Los Losers is an upcoming Secret Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot that Sammy leaked on Update 60, but it is not fully confirmed and may be a joke. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Los_Losers]
Los Losers, the unreleased Steal a Brainrot Secret, would show the three owners of the Roblox game 1+ Keyboard Escape. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Los_Losers]
A Speedrun limited time mode is listed as upcoming for Steal a Brainrot, hinted at in a Saturday Admin Abuse. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Upcoming_Content]
A Mining limited time mode is listed as upcoming for Steal a Brainrot, hinted at in Sari's Secret Admin Abuse. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Upcoming_Content]
The Fandom wiki lists an OG Lucky Block and an unknown OG Fuse Brainrot as unreleased Steal a Brainrot content based on hints from Sammy. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/OG]
Rhino Helicopterino and Centrucci Nuclucci are listed as confirmed upcoming Brainrot buffs in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Upcoming_Content]
Possible upcoming Steal a Brainrot buffs include Brutto Gialutto, Orbi Mochi, Money Money Man, Noo La Polizia, Jackorilla, Quackula, Boba Panda, and Bunnyman. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Upcoming_Content]
Sammy once announced that Steal a Brainrot players will soon be able to move their base to empty base spaces on the map. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Base]
Sammy once said a basement is coming to Steal a Brainrot bases. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Base]
The Fandom wiki lists a possible fourth Steal a Brainrot base floor for Rebirths 20 to 29, which is an estimate and not an announcement. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Base]
Sammy leaked that the Steal a Brainrot Taco Merchant will return with new Brainrots. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Taco_Merchant]
The Steal a Brainrot movie had no release date as of November 2026, and the Fandom wiki's late 2027 to mid-2028 window is a guess. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot_Movie]
Steal a Brainrot leaks appear in the community server's skibidi-leaks channel, from Sammy in Admin Abuses, or on YouTubers' live streams. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Leaks]
The first Steal a Brainrot leak on the Fandom wiki was La Grande Combinasion in June 2025. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Leaks]

## Scrapped content

Ice Dragon is a scrapped Steal a Brainrot Secret, a Dragon Cannelloni variant for Santa's Fuse that lost a poll to Dragon Gingerini. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Unused_Content]
Disco is a scrapped Steal a Brainrot mutation that Sammy leaked on March 11, 2026, and it might return later. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Disco_Event]
Disco was meant to follow the Divine mutation in Steal a Brainrot, but Sammy replaced it with Cyber after an idea from YouTuber Rustynickle40. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mutations]
Flappin Clover is a scrapped Secret Brainrot that was meant for the Steal a Brainrot Lucky Pot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Flappin_Clover]
Rainbow and Gold is a scrapped Steal a Brainrot Brainrot that was meant for the Leprechaun Lucky Block and was replaced by Cloverat Clapat. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Unused_Content]

## Records and milestones

Steal a Brainrot was the first game to pass 25 million concurrent players, in October 2025. [source: https://www.pocketgamer.biz/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-becomes-first-game-to-surpass-25m-concurrent-players/]
Steal a Brainrot was the second Roblox game to pass 20 million concurrent players, after Grow a Garden. [source: https://www.pocketgamer.biz/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-becomes-first-game-to-surpass-25m-concurrent-players/]
Steal a Brainrot passed the Grow a Garden concurrent player record of nearly 22.4 million in September 2025. [source: https://www.pocketgamer.biz/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-becomes-first-game-to-surpass-25m-concurrent-players/]
The Admin War between Steal a Brainrot and Grow a Garden took place in August 2025 and pushed Roblox past 45 million concurrent users by Roblox's own count, a platform record, with trackers reporting about 47 million. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/roblox-smashes-concurrent-user-record-thanks-to-war-between-two-games-3242035/] [source: https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1315098/000131509825000326/ex991-q32025shareholderl.htm | id=A16-0029 | type=official | status=confirmed | checked=2026-11-06]
The Extinct Event in September 2025 drove the Steal a Brainrot concurrent player peak that passed Grow a Garden. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot]
Steal a Brainrot had 1.1 billion lifetime visits by June 24, 2025, according to Know Your Meme. [source: https://knowyourmeme.com/memes/subcultures/steal-a-brainrot]
Steal a Brainrot held an event to celebrate 10 billion visits with Update 11 in August 2025. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_11]
Steal a Brainrot had 30.1 billion lifetime visits when it passed 25 million concurrent players in October 2025. [source: https://www.pocketgamer.biz/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-becomes-first-game-to-surpass-25m-concurrent-players/]
Steal a Brainrot had more than 55 billion visits by January 2026. [source: https://www.story-kitchen.com/news/story-kitchen-partnering-with-steal-a-brainrot-creators-on-feature-adaptation-of-roblox-game]
Steal a Brainrot had about 74.3 billion visits and about 29.7 million favorites on November 4, 2026. [source: https://games.roblox.com/v1/games?universeIds=7709344486]
Roblox's own Q3 2025 shareholder letter says Steal a Brainrot eclipsed 25 million concurrent players in October 2025. [source: https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1315098/000131509825000326/ex991-q32025shareholderl.htm | id=A16-0001 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | pub=2025-10-30 | event=2025-10 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The letter gives no exact peak figure. PocketGamer.biz reported 25.4 million on October 7, 2025; the exact peak is disputed (see the October conflict record).] [source: https://www.pocketgamer.biz/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-becomes-first-game-to-surpass-25m-concurrent-players/ | checked=2026-11-06]
Roblox's Q3 2025 shareholder letter says Grow a Garden set a world record of nearly 22 million concurrent players in July 2025, before Steal a Brainrot passed it. [source: https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1315098/000131509825000326/ex991-q32025shareholderl.htm | id=A16-0002 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | pub=2025-10-30 | event=2025-07 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The letter says this was the world record at the time, for most concurrent videogame players.]
Roblox's Q3 2025 shareholder letter says the Roblox platform had over 45 million concurrent users on August 23, 2025. [source: https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1315098/000131509825000326/ex991-q32025shareholderl.htm | id=A16-0003 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | pub=2025-10-30 | event=2025-08-23 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The letter's figure is a floor (over 45 million). Third-party trackers gave 47.3 to 47.4 million for the same day; those are not official.]
Roblox's Q3 2025 shareholder letter says Steal a Brainrot and 99 Nights in the Forest each reached concurrent peaks that would have exceeded Roblox's whole-platform peak of the prior year. [source: https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1315098/000131509825000326/ex991-q32025shareholderl.htm | id=A16-0004 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | pub=2025-10-30 | event=2025 | checked=2026-11-06 | note='Last year' is 2024, read from a letter written in 2025. The letter does not give a number for either game.]
Roblox said its Bruno Mars concert inside Steal a Brainrot on January 17, 2026 drew 12.8 million concurrent users. [source: https://www.pocketgamer.biz/roblox-reports-record-128m-concurrent-users-for-bruno-mars-virtual-concert | id=A16-0009 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | pub=2026-01-27 | event=2026-01-17 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=PocketGamer.biz relays Roblox's own announcement. Guinness World Records gives 12,862,161 concurrent viewers for the same event.] [source: https://www.guinnessworldrecords.com/news/commercial/2026/2/bruno-mars-hosts-largest-concert-in-a-videogame-during-steal-a-brainrot-on-roblox | checked=2026-11-06]
The Brunito Marsito brainrot released for the Bruno Mars concert in Steal a Brainrot was downloaded 5,428,644 times, according to Guinness World Records. [source: https://www.guinnessworldrecords.com/news/commercial/2026/2/bruno-mars-hosts-largest-concert-in-a-videogame-during-steal-a-brainrot-on-roblox | id=A16-0011 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | event=2026-01-17 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=PocketGamer.biz gives more than 5.4 million collected items for the same event.] [source: https://www.pocketgamer.biz/roblox-reports-record-128m-concurrent-users-for-bruno-mars-virtual-concert | checked=2026-11-06]
Steal a Brainrot had over 5.1 million favorites by late June 2025, according to RoMonitorStats data reported by Know Your Meme. [source: https://knowyourmeme.com/memes/subcultures/steal-a-brainrot | id=A16-0019 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-06 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Same RoMonitorStats chain as the June 2025 visit figure, so not independent of it.]
PocketGamer.biz said in October 2025 that the peak of Steal a Brainrot was a record for any single game, far above Fortnite's 15.3 million concurrent players from late 2020. [source: https://www.pocketgamer.biz/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-becomes-first-game-to-surpass-25m-concurrent-players/ | id=A16-0027 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | pub=2025-10-07 | event=2025-10 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Only PocketGamer.biz makes this record and Fortnite comparison. Roblox's letter supports the 25 million milestone but not the comparison.]

## Answers Sammy confirmed

# These come from answers SpyderSammy gave after his own riddles, in recordings of his Admin Abuse.
Scarino Crowino is a reskin of Fishino Clownino in Steal a Brainrot. [source: Sammy's answer FISHINOCLOWNINO15, recording 2026-11-05 13-27-35]
Steal a Brainrot released on May 15, so the day in May it released is 15. [source: Sammy's answer FISHINOCLOWNINO15, recording 2026-11-05 13-27-35]
Polaroidini is the rarest Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot besides Spyder Elephant, and 23 of it exist in total. [source: Sammy's answer POLAROIDINI23, recording 2026-11-05 13-33-07]
Sammy's favorite color is blue. [source: Sammy's answer BLUEBLUEBLUE67, recording 2026-11-05 13-34-03]
Tralalero Tralala is a shark. [source: answer to Sammy's riddle "what animal is tralalero", reported by the MASSAB user]
Sammy's favorite color is blue and his name is Sammy. [source: accepted answer bluesammy67, recording 2026-11-05 21-06-18]
Sammy's favorite Travis Scott album is Astroworld. [source: answer ASTROWORLD777 typed in recording 2026-11-05 21-07-03]
The most op minifigure toy in the new Series 2 minifigures is the Griffin. [source: Sammy's answer GRIFFIN10, recording 2026-11-05 21-05-26]

## Riddles and Admin Abuse history

In Steal a Brainrot, the riddle code CHIMPANZINISPIDERINI asked for the former name of Sammyni Spyderini and rewarded a Pum Pum Pum. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Chimpanzini_Spiderini | id=A20-0001 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki does not give the riddle wording or the date. The wiki's Chimpanzini Spiderini page says this riddle named the former name of Sammyni Spyderini, which was Chimpanzini Spiderini.]
The riddle code MAYMEOWLJANDEL in Steal a Brainrot rewarded a Meowl and had a limited stock of three. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Codes | id=A20-0002 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki does not state the riddle wording. Reading the answer as my favorite Brainrot plus Jandel is an inference: the Meowl page says Meowl is Sammy's favorite Brainrot, and the MASSAB bench examples show spoken my tra]
The riddle code CANDY24SAMMY in Steal a Brainrot rewarded a Strawberry Elephant and had a limited stock of one. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Codes | id=A20-0003 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki does not state the riddle wording, so what the answer encodes is unknown.]
The riddle code DIVINECURSED in Steal a Brainrot rewarded a Strawberry Elephant and had a limited stock of one. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Codes | id=A20-0004 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki writes the stock as -1, which is probably a typo for one. The wiki does not state the riddle wording.]
On October 17, 2026, Sammy posted a Strawberry Elephant riddle asking for the height of the tree in Sammy's backyard, the wattage of Sammy's microwave, and the name of Sammy's 4th grade math teacher, and the Fandom wiki listed it as unsolved. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Strawberry_Elephant | id=A20-0005 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-10-17 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki says the riddle has not been solved as of its last edit, so the unsolved status is time-sensitive. The riddle's personal content is left out on purpose.]
According to Sammy, the best mutation to use on a Meowl in Steal a Brainrot is Cyber, and the best trait to use is Strawberry, for how it looks. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Meowl | id=A20-0006 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Visual preference only, as stated on the wiki. The wiki does not give the date Sammy said it.]
Sammyni Spyderini was the first Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot that Sammy spawned during an Admin Abuse and that players could then obtain. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Family | id=A20-0008 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki does not give a date for this first spawn.]
Sammy spawned Raccooni Jandelini in Steal a Brainrot on August 23, 2025 and again on August 24, 2025, during the Admin Abuse War. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Family | id=A20-0009 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-08-23 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki says the Brainrot was made to poke fun at Jandel, the owner of Grow a Garden.]
Raccooni Jandelini was stocked in the Cyber Craft Machine in Steal a Brainrot on the May 5, 2026 Taco Tuesday, with 1K units at four Noobini Pizzaninis each, and sold out in under a minute. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Family | id=A20-0010 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-05-05 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Both the stock and the sell-out time come from the wiki only.]
Sammy spawned La Karkerkar Combinasion on purpose during the October 14, 2025 Taco Tuesday in Steal a Brainrot, because its Craft Machine had been replaced by the Witch Fuse. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Abuse | id=A20-0011 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-10-14 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki says that after this, Sammy nerfed the craft recipe so it needs only two Karkerkar Kurkurs. The September 9, 2025 spawn was a mistake.]
Sammy secretly added Polaroidini to Steal a Brainrot on the August 11, 2026 Taco Tuesday, without telling anyone. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Taco_Tuesday | id=A20-0012 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-08-11 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Polaroidini is a DLC Brainrot according to its wiki infobox.]
In a Steal a Brainrot poll on Taco Tuesday, April 14, 2026, Centrucci Nuclucci beat Rhino Helicopterino by about 200K votes for a buff. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Centrucci_Nuclucci | id=A20-0014 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-04-14 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Rhino Helicopterino page reports the same poll. Both are wiki pages, so they are not independent.]
On March 17, 2026, a Steal a Brainrot poll on nerfing Garama and Madundung drew 2.2 million votes for the nerf, but the nerf was never applied. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Garama_and_Madundung | id=A20-0015 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-03-17 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki says a second poll on March 21, 2026 also voted for a nerf, and that the nerf never happened.]
In a December 15, 2025 Steal a Brainrot community poll on its rarity, players chose to make Skibidi Toilet an OG Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Skibidi_Toilet | id=A20-0016 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-12-15 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki says many people argued it should not be an OG because it was made in 2023. It was released as OG on December 27, 2025 per the update log.]
In a Steal a Brainrot poll, Dragon Gingerini beat Ice Dragon, and Ice Dragon was scrapped. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Dragon_Gingerini | id=A20-0017 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Ice Dragon page says it was teased in the December 13th, 2025 Admin Abuse and lost the poll. The poll date is not given.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Ice_Dragon | checked=2026-11-06]
Granny in Steal a Brainrot was added after a poll in which a majority voted yes, and Sammy announced it would release. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Granny | id=A20-0018 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki gives no poll date. Granny was added in Update 43 on March 21, 2026 per the update log.]
Job Job Job Sahur was added to Steal a Brainrot on August 15, 2025 because it won a poll in the game's official Discord server. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Job_Job_Job_Sahur | id=A20-0019 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-08-15 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki says the poll was in the Discord server, not on X.]
Tartaruga Cisterna came second in the August 10, 2025 Steal a Brainrot poll, behind Job Job Job Sahur. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Tartaruga_Cisterna | id=A20-0020 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-08-10 | checked=2026-11-06]
Sammy teased the Steal a Brainrot Trade Machine in a poll on the February 17, 2026 Taco Tuesday, and the majority of voters agreed to its arrival. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Trade_Machine | id=A20-0021 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-02-17 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki also says Sammy announced it as a poll on January 22, 2026.]
In a Steal a Brainrot poll asking who is bigger, Steak beat Capitano Moby. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Capitano_Moby | id=A20-0022 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki gives no date for this poll.]
La Easter Grande in Steal a Brainrot was added after a Sammy poll in which most voters said yes to adding it. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/La_Easter_Grande | id=A20-0023 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Sammy leaked it after the poll and said it would appear on Tuesday. The wiki gives no poll date.]

Buntteo is an Easter reskin of Matteo in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Buntteo | id=A03-0007 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]

Fragrama and Chocrama is a reskin of Garama and Madundung in Steal a Brainrot, sold by the Brainrot Trader. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Fragrama_and_Chocrama | id=A03-0012 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Karkerheart Luvkur is a Brainrot God reskin of Karkerkar Kurkur in Steal a Brainrot, added for the Valentine's Update. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Karkerheart_Luvkur | id=A03-0013 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]

Esok Goala is a sport-themed reskin of Esok Sekolah in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Esok_Goala | id=A03-0015 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]

Capitano Moby's former name in Steal a Brainrot was Whalnero. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Capitano_Moby | id=A03-0026 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Elefanto Frigo is named Elefantino Frigorifero in its original meme, but the in-game name differs. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Elefanto_Frigo | id=A03-0027 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Tung Tung Tung Sahur used to have the biggest buff in Steal a Brainrot, with its income raised 60,000 times and its cost raised 160,000 times. Headless Horseman is the first OG Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot that comes from a machine, the Witch Fuse. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Headless_Horseman | id=A03-0031 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Headless Horseman's income was raised from $175M/s to $200M/s in the Gingerbread Town update on December 20, 2025, and from $200M/s to $325M/s on January 24, 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Headless_Horseman | id=A03-0032 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | event=2026-01-24 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Sportskeeda's January 31, 2026 Secret list shows Headless Horseman at 325M per second, which matches the value after the January 24 buff.] [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/all-secrets-steal-brainrot | checked=2026-11-06]
Headless Horseman was buffed to $600M/s in the Anniversary Update on May 23, 2026 in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Headless_Horseman | id=A03-0033 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-05-23 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki lists the earlier step to $550M/s at the Divine Fuse update on March 7, 2026.]
Headless Horseman was made an OG Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot in Update 40, at $500M/s. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Jacko_Jakat | id=A03-0056 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-10-31 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Headless Merchant ran from October 31 to November 1, 2026 on the wiki.]
The wiki's count of Headless Horseman in existence is 74, after a developer leak said about 75. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Headless_Horseman | id=A03-0057 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki gives no date for this count. Earlier, a SpyderSammy statement said there were not 138 and that there were under 100, and later about 75.]
Los Lucky Blocks contain 8 Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot, with Los Bombinitos the most common at 40% and Los Sekolahs the rarest at 0.25%. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Los_Lucky_Blocks | id=A03-0059 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Los Puggies were released in Steal a Brainrot on November 8, 2025 as the Los version of Money Money Puggy. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Money_Money_Puggy | id=A03-0060 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-11-08 | checked=2026-11-06]
Los Bros can be crafted in Steal a Brainrot's Craft Machine from Los Tungtungtungcitos, Los Tralaleritos, La Grande Combinasion and Los Combinasionas. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Los_Bros | id=A03-0061 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]

## Lucky Blocks

The Mythic Lucky Block costs 2.5M cash in Steal a Brainrot, and the wiki calls it the cheapest Lucky Block by far. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mythic_Lucky_Block | id=A11-0001 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Cash price for the conveyor belt. Sportskeeda (modified Sep 30, 2025) and Beebom (Nov 27, 2025) also list 2.5 million cash, so this may have changed since those dates.] [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-lucky-blocks-all-lucky-blocks-price-list | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-lucky-blocks-guide | checked=2026-11-06]

Beebom (Nov 27, 2025) also lists 175 Robux. Sportskeeda (modified Sep 30, 2025) lists 149 Robux, an older price.] [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-lucky-blocks-guide | checked=2026-11-06]

[source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Lucky_Pot | id=A11-0039 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-03-14 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki says the Lucky Pot triggers its St Patrick's event every two hours. Cloverat Clapat makes $60M/s and costs $15B, per the wiki.]
Bananito Bandito is the rarest Brainrot from the Mythic Lucky Block in Steal a Brainrot, at a 3% chance. Pop Pop Sahur, at a 1% chance, is the rarest Brainrot in the Brainrot God Lucky Block in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Brainrot_God_Lucky_Block | id=A11-0041 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Beebom (Nov 27, 2025) also gives Pop Pop Sahur 1%. Sportskeeda's Sep 2025 list gives different odds, so the wiki odds are the ones to trust.] [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-lucky-blocks-guide | checked=2026-11-06]
The Heart Lucky Block costs 799 Robux in the Steal a Brainrot Robux Shop, per the wiki as of November 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Heart_Lucky_Block | id=A11-0042 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Only the wiki infobox gives this Robux price. The wiki lists the Heart block as unobtainable.]
The Premium Festive Lucky Block costs 799 Robux in the Steal a Brainrot Christmas Shop, per the wiki. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Festive_Lucky_Block | id=A11-0043 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki says the Christmas Shop sold it for 799, 2,399 for three, and 7,999 for ten, and that the Premium version differs from the normal block only in how it is bought.]
The Egg Lucky Block costs 799 Robux in Steal a Brainrot, per the wiki. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Egg_Lucky_Block | id=A11-0044 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Only the wiki infobox gives this Robux price. The Leprechaun Lucky Block costs 649 Robux in the Steal a Brainrot Robux Shop, per the wiki. ## Gear and items

The coin shop in Steal a Brainrot sells gears for in-game Cash, and the items it offers change with your rebirth level. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Shop | id=A09-0001 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The basic Slap costs $500 in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot and needs no rebirths. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Slap | id=A09-0003 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Price also listed as 500 Cash by Sportskeeda (July 2025 article) and as $500 by TechWiser.] [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/all-steal-brainrot-weapons | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Gold Slap costs 15,000 Cash in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot and requires 2 rebirths. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/all-steal-brainrot-weapons | id=A09-0004 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | pub=2025-07-26 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Sportskeeda article dated July 26, 2025; TechWiser and the Fandom Slap page give the same price and rebirth count.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Slap | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/roblox-steal-a-brainrot-gears-tier-list/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Speed Coil costs $750 in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs no rebirths, and raises move speed by 50 percent, from 34 to 51. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0006 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Gravity Coil costs $3,000 in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs 1 rebirth, and doubles jump height from 7.2 to 14.4. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0007 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Coil Combo costs $20,000 in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs 2 rebirths, and gives the effects of the Speed and Gravity Coils at the same time. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0008 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Trap costs $1,000 in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs no rebirths, freezes a thief for 7 seconds, and each player can hold or place up to 5. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0009 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Bee Launcher costs $10,000 in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs 1 rebirth, and reverses the target's controls and tints their screen yellow for 5 seconds. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0010 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Taser Gun costs $100,000 in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs 3 rebirths, and ragdolls the hit player for 3 seconds. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0011 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/roblox-steal-a-brainrot-gears-tier-list/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Invisibility Cloak costs $300,000 in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs 4 rebirths, and makes you invisible while raising your speed from 34 to 68. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0012 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The effect lasts until the item is used again or unequipped.] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Boogie Bomb costs $500,000 in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs 4 rebirths, and makes players in its blast drop held Brainrots and dance for 10 seconds. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0013 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
Medusa's Head costs $5,000,000 in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs 5 rebirths, and turns other players in its area into stone for 3 seconds. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0014 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Web Slinger costs $2,000,000 in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs 6 rebirths, and tangles a hit player so they cannot move for 10 seconds. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0015 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Heart Balloon costs $150M in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs 12 rebirths, and lets you float upward for 10 seconds. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0018 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=A Sportskeeda article dated July 2025 lists 15,000,000 Cash, which does not match the Fandom and TechWiser figure.] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Magnet costs 200,000,000 Cash in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot and needs 12 rebirths. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/all-steal-brainrot-weapons | id=A09-0019 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | pub=2025-07-26 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Sportskeeda article dated July 26, 2025. TechWiser and the Fandom Gears page give the same price and rebirth count.] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | checked=2026-11-06]
The Giant Potion costs $1.5 billion in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs 17 rebirths, and makes the drinker about four times bigger for 20 seconds. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0020 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The size effect is from the Fandom wiki only.] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Attack Doge costs $1 billion in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot and needs 16 rebirths, and its dog disappears after 11 seconds if no one destroys it. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0021 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Grief Shield, the Rebirth 19 gear in Steal a Brainrot, absorbs one hit from a griefer before it breaks and does not apply to the player whose Brainrot you are stealing. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_62 | id=A09-0022 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Settles the Needs review conflict over the name. The Rebirth table on the Fandom Rebirth page and Beebom say Grief Guard. Fandom Update 62, the Fandom Gears page, allthings.how, bloxodes, MitchCactus and U7BUY all say Gr] [source: https://www.u7buy.com/blog/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-19/ | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://allthings.how/steal-a-brainrot-rebirth-19-guide-requirements-and-rewards/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Laser Cape costs $20 million in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs 9 rebirths, and lets you see invisible players and shoot lasers from your eyes. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0025 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/roblox-steal-a-brainrot-gears-tier-list/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Paintball Gun costs $100 million in the coin shop of Steal a Brainrot, needs 11 rebirths, and cuts a hit player's speed by 60 percent. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0026 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://techwiser.com/roblox-steal-a-brainrot-gears-tier-list/ | checked=2026-11-06]
In Steal a Brainrot the Robux Shop sells Blackhole Slap for 199 Robux, Laser Gun for 749 Robux, and Ban Hammer for 1,499 Robux. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Robux_Shop | id=A09-0027 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Sportskeeda (July 2025) lists Laser Gun at 999 Robux and Ban Hammer with a typo price. The Fandom Robux Shop page also lists a Black Friday discount (see separate record).] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Flying Carpet costs 375 Robux in the Robux Shop of Steal a Brainrot and lets the owner fly. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Robux_Shop | id=A09-0028 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Flying Carpet is not among the game passes returned by the Roblox game-pass API on the same date, so it may be a developer product.] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://www.u7buy.com/blog/flying-gears-steal-a-brainrot/ | checked=2026-11-06]
Witch's Broom is a flying gear sold for 499 Robux in Steal a Brainrot, and the wiki lists it as a Halloween limited item. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Robux_Shop | id=A09-0029 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Listed under Limited/Removed Items on the Fandom Robux Shop page, so it may not be buyable now.] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://www.u7buy.com/blog/flying-gears-steal-a-brainrot/ | checked=2026-11-06]
Santa's Sleigh is a flying gear that costs 1,799 Robux in the Robux Shop of Steal a Brainrot and drops presents. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Shop | id=A09-0030 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Listed under Limited/Removed Items on the Fandom pages, so it may not be buyable now.] [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://www.u7buy.com/blog/flying-gears-steal-a-brainrot/ | checked=2026-11-06]
During the Black Friday 2025 sale in Steal a Brainrot, the Laser Gun was discounted from 749 Robux to 649 Robux. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Robux_Shop | id=A09-0032 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-11-30 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The sale ran from November 30, 2025 to December 1, 2025 according to the Fandom Robux Shop page. The Flying Carpet and Ban Hammer were also discounted.]
Fishing rods in Steal a Brainrot can only be used during the fishing event. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Unobtainable_gears | id=A09-0035 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-fishing-guide/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Frozen Rod in Steal a Brainrot costs 2.5 million Cash, needs 5 rebirths, and has 2x cast luck. [source: https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-fishing-guide/ | id=A09-0036 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Fandom Unobtainable gears page gives the same price, rebirth count and 2x luck.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Unobtainable_gears | checked=2026-11-06]
The Agarrini Shovel was the first gear in Steal a Brainrot that helps players get Brainrots, and it is tied to a specific event. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0039 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Fandom Gears page says the Shovel and the Fishing Rods are Event Exclusive gears tied to specific events.]
The Divine Slap comes from the Divine spin wheel with a 0.25 percent chance in Steal a Brainrot, and makes the hit player levitate before dropping them. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Slap | id=A09-0040 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Event wheel gear. Wheel odds are from the Fandom wiki only.]
The Yin Yang Slap comes from the Yin Yang spin wheel with a 0.5 percent chance in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://techwiser.com/all-items-in-steal-a-brainrot-roblox/ | id=A09-0041 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Fandom Slap page gives the same odds during the YinYang event.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Slap | checked=2026-11-06]
A player in Steal a Brainrot can change the skin of their base at any time in the game settings. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Base | id=A09-0043 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Bat is the default gear every player receives when joining Steal a Brainrot, and it was formerly called Tung Bat. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gears | id=A09-0044 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The rename happened after Tung Tung Tung Sahur was removed, according to the Fandom Gears page.]

## Gamepasses and monetization

The Admin Panel in Steal a Brainrot was raised from 1,999 Robux to 4,999 Robux in an overhaul on July 12, 2025. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Admin_Panel | id=A08-0004 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-07-12 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The same page says it was later changed to 7,499 Robux, with no date given. Current price is disputed (see the Admin Panel conflict record).]
The 2x Server Luck product in the Robux Shop of Steal a Brainrot costs 249 Robux and lasts 15 minutes. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Robux_Shop | id=A08-0008 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Wiki Robux Shop page last edited 2026-09-06. The wiki Gamepasses page gives the same 249 Robux price.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gamepasses_and_Dev_Products | checked=2026-11-06]
The Blackhole Starter Pack in Steal a Brainrot costs 189 Robux and grants a permanent Blackhole slap, a permanent coil combo, $1,000, and a Brr Brr Patapim. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Robux_Shop | id=A08-0010 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki Gamepasses page lists the current price as 189 Robux, against a worth of 499. Wiki Robux Shop page last edited 2026-09-06.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gamepasses_and_Dev_Products | checked=2026-11-06]
Steal a Brainrot's Black Friday sale on Robux gears took place on November 30, 2025 and ended on December 1, 2025. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Robux_Shop | id=A08-0013 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-11-30 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=During the sale the wiki lists Laser Gun 749 to 649 Robux, Flying Carpet 375 to 299 Robux, and Ban Hammer 1,499 to 1,199 Robux.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Shop | checked=2026-11-06]
The 2x Money gamepass in Steal a Brainrot applies a 2x multiplier to base earnings, so a 14x base becomes 16x rather than doubling total earnings. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Robux_Shop | id=A08-0014 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Wiki footnote on the Robux Shop page gives the 14x example.]
The VIP gamepass in Steal a Brainrot adds a 0.5x cash multiplier to base earnings, a VIP chat tag, and an extra 10 seconds of base lockdown. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Robux_Shop | id=A08-0015 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Wiki footnote on the Robux Shop page. The wiki Base page also says VIP gives a fixed 10 seconds of lock time. Price is disputed (see the VIP conflict record).] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Base | checked=2026-11-06]
The Robux Shop in Steal a Brainrot has a Gift Player option, which makes purchases apply to another player in the same server. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Robux_Shop | id=A08-0016 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Wiki Robux Shop page, Gifting section.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Shop | checked=2026-11-06]
In Steal a Brainrot, one Wheel spin costs 99 Robux, a discounted daily spin costs 49 Robux, and three spins cost 199 Robux, according to the wiki. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Wheels | id=A08-0017 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Wiki Wheels page last edited 2026-10-23. The 49 Robux daily price is described as a discount.]

## Codes and promotions

The Steal a Brainrot codes feature was added on December 13, 2025. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Codes | id=A07-0002 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-12-13 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki trivia only. No second source checked.]
The first Steal a Brainrot code ever added was for the Festive 67 brainrot and plushie. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Codes | id=A07-0003 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki trivia only.]
In Steal a Brainrot, codes were moved from the Shop menu to a dedicated Codes button on June 13, 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Codes | id=A07-0004 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | event=2026-06-13 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki gives the exact date. GamesRadar (updated 2026-11-04) says the change happened as of June 2026 and that the Codes button replaced the Shop menu route.] [source: https://www.gamesradar.com/games/simulation/roblox-steal-a-brainrot-codes | checked=2026-11-06]
To redeem a Steal a Brainrot code, click the Codes button on the left side of the screen, type the code into the box and press Submit. [source: https://techwiser.com/roblox-steal-a-brainrot-codes/ | id=A07-0005 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | pub=2026-10-01 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The page was last updated 2026-10-01. Its original publish date is 2025-06-26. The same steps appear in GamesRadar and PocketGamer.] [source: https://www.gamesradar.com/games/simulation/roblox-steal-a-brainrot-codes | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://www.pocketgamer.com/roblox/steal-a-brainrot-codes/ | checked=2026-11-06]
Steal a Brainrot codes are not case-sensitive, so they can be typed in lowercase. [source: https://www.gamesradar.com/games/simulation/roblox-steal-a-brainrot-codes | id=A07-0006 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | pub=2026-11-04 | checked=2026-11-06]
A redeemed Steal a Brainrot code spawns its brainrot on the Red Carpet in your server instead of adding it to your collection. [source: https://www.gamesradar.com/games/simulation/roblox-steal-a-brainrot-codes | id=A07-0007 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | pub=2026-11-04 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The brainrot can then be bought or lost to another player, so the player has to have the money ready before redeeming.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Festive_67 | checked=2026-11-06]
The Festive 67 Plush sold on the official Steal a Brainrot site for $29.99, and the site shows it as sold out as of November 2026. [source: https://playsab.com | id=A07-0010 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The official store page showed Sold Out when read on 2026-11-06. The item is sold as an emailed DLC code, see the related facts.]
The Boppin Bunny Plush sold on the official Steal a Brainrot site for $39.99, and the site shows it as sold out as of November 2026. [source: https://playsab.com | id=A07-0011 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The official store page showed Sold Out and a preorder shipping date of 05/30/26 when read on 2026-11-06.]
Steal a Brainrot plush merchandise comes with an emailed in-game code rather than a physical code, according to the official store. [source: https://playsab.com | id=A07-0012 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Stated on the official store listing for the Boppin Bunny Plush.]
The Steal a Brainrot plush DLC codes redeem for an exclusive in-game item, according to the official store. [source: https://playsab.com | id=A07-0013 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Stated on the official store listings for the Festive 67 Plush and the Boppin Bunny Plush.]
Official Steal a Brainrot merchandise such as the Festive 67 plush is officially licensed and made by PhatMojo. [source: https://playsab.com | id=A07-0014 | type=official | status=confirmed | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Stated on the official store listing for the Festive 67 Plush.]
Randomly generated Steal a Brainrot DLC codes can give one reward from a pool that includes Pineaplino, Lazy Ducky, Tenini Ballini, Globa Steppa and Rico Dinero. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Codes | id=A07-0015 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki table only. The pool is not confirmed by the developer. The wiki's MerchShop DLC page gives the same reward names for the DLC tiers.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/MerchShop_DLC | checked=2026-11-06]
Each Festive 67 code in Steal a Brainrot is randomly generated because a payment is required to get the Festive 67. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Codes | id=A07-0016 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki trivia only.]
Sammy paused making new Steal a Brainrot codes from June 27 to June 30, 2026, during an Admin Abuse event, because of exploiters.
