## Trading and economy

SpyderSammy first teased the Trade Machine in Steal a Brainrot through a community poll on January 22, 2026. [source: https://techwiser.com/steal-a-brainrot-trade-machine/ | id=A12-0007 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | pub=2026-02-22 | event=2026-01-22 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Trade Machine wiki page also gives the poll as 1.22.2026 (January 22, 2026). The same article reports over 20,000 votes in favour, which is not recorded here.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Trade_Machine | checked=2026-11-06]
Before the Trade Machine, Steal a Brainrot trades often required one player to hand over a Brainrot first, and the taker then locked their base, which often led to scams. [source: https://beebom.com/how-to-trade-in-steal-a-brainrot/ | id=A12-0008 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | pub=2026-02-22 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Beebom describes the pre-machine scam pattern. TechWiser says that players trading outside the game were often scammed. Neither is an official statement.] [source: https://techwiser.com/steal-a-brainrot-trade-machine/ | checked=2026-11-06]
The Trade Machine in Steal a Brainrot lets players mark the Brainrots they are looking for, shown as LF. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Trade_Machine | id=A12-0009 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki trivia. The LF feature date is not given.]
In Steal a Brainrot, a script once let scammers keep the Brainrots that players put into Trade Machine trades, and the wiki says that script was later patched. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Trade_Machine | id=A12-0010 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki only. No date is given for the script or the patch. The method is not recorded here.]
A scam in Steal a Brainrot had players put mutations on Mythic Lucky Blocks and claim they were Secret or OG Lucky Blocks. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mythic_Lucky_Block | id=A12-0011 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki says the scam was going around at the time of writing and gives no date. Undated.]
A fake Steal a Brainrot ritual using 2 Lirilì Larilà and 2 Strawberrelli Flamingelli was spread by scammers, and it never spawned a Strawberry Elephant. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rituals | id=A12-0012 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki; the scam is undated.]
In Steal a Brainrot, selling a Brainrot from your own base returns half of its original price in cash. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Cash | id=A12-0018 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki only. Original price is as the wiki uses it; not checked against the game.]
Garama and Madundung is the unofficial currency of Steal a Brainrot trades, and many Brainrot values are based on it, as of November 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Garama_and_Madundung | id=A12-0019 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki label. The game has no official trading currency, and no official source was found for this label.]
Exist Counts, which show how many of each Brainrot exist in Steal a Brainrot, were added in Update 56. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Exist_Counts | id=A12-0022 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki. The page does not give the Update 56 date. It says the counts appear in the Index, the Trade Machine, bases and the Trade Plaza book.]
Noobini Pizzanini has the highest exist count of any Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot, according to the community wiki as of November 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Noobini_Pizzanini | id=A12-0023 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki Exist Counts table (https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Exist_Counts, header dated June 2026) lists 439,477,976 for it. Exist counts are leaked or released by the developer and change over time.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Exist_Counts | checked=2026-11-06]
SpyderSammy's leaked exist counts for Gorillo Subwoofero in Steal a Brainrot showed 9,585 exists, as of July 7, 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gorillo_Subwoofero | id=A12-0024 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-07-07 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki. The same page says it makes it the rarest Mythic in the game by the wiki's own ranking, which is not recorded here.]

## Maps, NPCs and limited time modes

Most Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot come out of the Cave, the map's entrance, and walk down the Red Carpet to be bought. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Red_Carpet | id=A13-0001 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Live Spawns panel in Steal a Brainrot stands next to the Red Carpet entrance and shows globally announced OG Brainrot spawns. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Red_Carpet | id=A13-0002 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Live Spawns Board page says it shows the most recent OG spawns with the spawning player's display name.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Live_Spawns_Board | checked=2026-11-06]
The timetables that show Steal a Brainrot's hourly seasonal events stand next to the Robux Shop. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Hourly_Events | id=A13-0003 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
During the Fishing event in Steal a Brainrot, the Fisherman NPC spawned near the Gear Shop, where players could buy Fishing Rods for more luck. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Fishing | id=A13-0005 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Fishing page says the event is now only run during Admin Abuse.]
The Fishing Event in Steal a Brainrot was revealed on November 14, 2025 at 4:30PM EST. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Fishing_Event | id=A13-0006 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The reveal was on the Community Server and on Roblox. This is a reveal date, not the date the event ran.]
Only three Brainrots have been confirmed from the rare puddles in the Steal a Brainrot Fishing event: Tralaledon, Orcaledon and Capitano Moby. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Fishing | id=A13-0007 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Fishing event in Steal a Brainrot is no longer run every hour and is now only run during Admin Abuse, according to the Fandom wiki. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Fishing | id=A13-0008 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=As of November 2026. The page says the event formerly occurred every hour.]
Players entered the Escape Tsunami limited time mode in Steal a Brainrot through the LTM Event Machine, then chose a server from its menu. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Escape_Tsunami_LTM | id=A13-0009 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-01-24 | checked=2026-11-06]
The Arcadopus NPC in Steal a Brainrot was the gateway to the Escape Tsunami limited time mode, where players could interact with it to access the mode. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Arcadopus | id=A13-0010 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Escape Tsunami page describes the LTM Event Machine as the entrance, so the two may both apply.]
The Escape Tsunami limited time mode in Steal a Brainrot had a Speed Shop that sold +1, +5 and +10 Speed upgrades for cash or Robux. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Escape_Tsunami_LTM | id=A13-0011 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Cash prices rise with each upgrade bought, while the Robux prices stay the same.]
The Escape Tsunami limited time mode lane in Steal a Brainrot had eight segments, rising from Common Brainrots to High-Tier Secret Brainrots. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Escape_Tsunami_LTM | id=A13-0012 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
A Speed Shop is added each time The Backrooms Event is held in Steal a Brainrot, and players use it to upgrade their speed for the Backrooms. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Backrooms_Event | id=A13-0013 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Backrooms Event in Steal a Brainrot offers only Brainrot God New and Secret Brainrots, unlike Escape Tsunami, which covered every rarity below OG. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Backrooms_Event | id=A13-0014 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-05-09 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The page says the Backrooms Event was added on Saturday, May 9th in Update 50.]
Lumaca Malefica, Robo Grafito, Glaciator, Flipa Sandala, Abyssaloco and Rubrikiko are listed as Backrooms Event exclusive Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Backrooms_Event | id=A13-0015 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The page marks each of these as a Backrooms Event Exclusive Brainrot.]
Ballerino Lololo, Skull Skull Skull, Garama and Madundung, and Hydra Dragon Cannelloni were not carried over from Escape Tsunami into the Backrooms Event in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Backrooms_Event | id=A13-0016 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Jump for Eggs in Steal a Brainrot has seven islands with five eggs each, and players unlock new islands with XP earned on trampolines near the base. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Jump_for_Eggs_LTM | id=A13-0018 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The islands are Grass/Starter, Desert, Arctic, Cave, Aquatic, Lava and Heavenly.]
Sammy said in April 2026 that the Eggrot Hunt could become a limited time mode, and Sammy later made it the Jump for Eggs limited time mode on September 26, 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Eggrot_Hunt | id=A13-0019 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-09-26 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Eggrot Hunt page quotes Sammy's April 10 comment and says he made the LTM on 9/26/26.]
Eggrot Hunt was an Easter event in Steal a Brainrot released on April 4, 2026 that had five layers, and players had to upgrade their jumps to climb them. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Eggrot_Hunt | id=A13-0020 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-04-04 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The infobox lists an end date of April 18, 2026.]
Egg City in Steal a Brainrot was a recurring map-wide Easter event that ran every 3 to 6 hours, with gold eggs hidden inside houses along the map borders. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Egg_City | id=A13-0021 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-04-04 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The infobox lists April 4 to April 18, 2026.]
In Egg City in Steal a Brainrot, a Quackini Snackini can spawn inside a house in place of a gold egg, with a 0.01% chance. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Egg_City | id=A13-0022 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-04-04 | checked=2026-11-06]
The Egg Incubator in Steal a Brainrot was released with Part 2 of Sammy's Easter update and lets players store eggs and Egg Lucky Blocks to get traits on them. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Egg_Incubator | id=A13-0023 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Update 45 log dates Easter Part 2 to April 4, 2026.]
Gingerbread Town in Steal a Brainrot started on December 20, 2025 and ended on January 3, 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gingerbread_Town | id=A13-0024 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-12-20 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The page's infobox gives both dates.]
In Gingerbread Town in Steal a Brainrot, players rode Santa's Sleigh and collected candy canes from rings in the sky. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gingerbread_Town | id=A13-0025 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Gingerbread Town in Steal a Brainrot gave only a very small chance of Money Money Reindeer. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gingerbread_Town | id=A13-0026 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The North Pole Event in Steal a Brainrot began on December 13, 2025 with a Brainrot Express train that took players to a snowy North Pole map. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/North_Pole_Event | id=A13-0027 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-12-13 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The page's infobox ends the event in early January 2026, and the page says it released in Update 29.]
The Trick or Treat event in Steal a Brainrot turned the map into a dark night scene with a blocky haunted mansion at the center. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Trick_Or_Treat | id=A13-0028 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-10-31 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The infobox lists October 31 to November 1, 2026.]
The Headless Merchant in Steal a Brainrot sold Spooky Kart for 1k Candy Corns or 499 Robux, Jacko Jakat for 3.5k or 999 Robux, Scorpi Inferni for 7.5k or 2,499 Robux, and Puppetmaster for 12.5k or 7,999 Robux. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Headless_Merchant | id=A13-0029 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-10-31 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Candy Corns come from the Trick or Treat event. The page also lists a stock limit for each item.]
The Headless Merchant was the fourth limited shop of its kind in Steal a Brainrot, after Santa's Shop, the Bee Shop and the Taco Merchant. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Headless_Merchant | id=A13-0030 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Taco Merchant in Steal a Brainrot sold Burrito Bat for 100 tacos, Tacoturbo Tacorito for 300, Nachorilla for 1,000 and Sammyni Truckini for 2,500 on its August 18, 2026 run. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Taco_Merchant | id=A13-0031 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-08-18 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The same page says Tacoturbo Tacorito was cut from 500 tacos to 300.]
The Taco Truck in Steal a Brainrot is a truck that sometimes appears during Admin Abuse and trades specific Brainrots for limited quantity Brainrots. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Taco_Truck | id=A13-0032 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Taco Truck first appeared in Steal a Brainrot on September 30, 2025, during Taco Tuesday, with Perrito Burrito and Chillin Chili as limited quantity Brainrots. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Taco_Tuesday | id=A13-0033 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-09-30 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The history table lists this as Taco Tuesday 7.]
The Bee Shop in Steal a Brainrot was a limited-time machine released with Update 61 that sold five Secret Brainrots for Honey or Robux. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bee_Shop | id=A13-0034 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The page lists a Honey cost and a Robux cost for each item.]
The Bee Shop's five Secret Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot were Conetto Morsetto, Honey Honey Bear, Queen Bee, S'more Serat and Bumbatron. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bee_Shop | id=A13-0035 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Santa's Shop in Steal a Brainrot was a limited-time machine that sold four Secret Brainrots: Los Jolly Combinasionas, Money Money Reindeer, Jolly Jolly Sahur and Ginger Gerat. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Santa's_Shop | id=A13-0037 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The page lists a Gold Elf cost and a Robux cost for each item.]
Gold Elves were the in-game currency for Santa's Shop in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gold_Elf | id=A13-0038 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-12-24 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The page says Gold Elf was added on 12/24/25 during Christmas Eve.]
Santa's Shop was added in Steal a Brainrot along with Santa's Fuse during the Christmas event. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Christmas | id=A13-0039 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Christmas page gives a start of November 30, 2025 and says it was part of Update 27, while the Update 27 log dates that update to November 29, 2025.]
Live Match Events was a Steal a Brainrot machine added with Update 55 to celebrate the 2026 FIFA World Cup, and it spawned Brainrots during live matches. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Live_Match_Events | id=A13-0040 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The page uses past tense, so the machine appears to be gone.]
The Live Match Events machine in Steal a Brainrot gave Hippo Golazo for a goal, Esok Goala for a goal with a small chance, Ref Ref Ref Sahur for a red card, and Var Var Var for a VAR check. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Live_Match_Events | id=A13-0041 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Los Traders in Steal a Brainrot was a variant of the Brainrot Trader with three purple trader NPCs. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Los_Traders | id=A13-0042 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Update 57 log lists Los Traders with six new Brainrots, and the Update 61 log says it was removed.]
The Brainrot Dealer stood for about one week in Steal a Brainrot, which the Fandom wiki calls the shortest time any machine has lasted. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Brainrot_Dealer | id=A13-0043 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-11-08 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The page says it replaced the Witch Fuse on November 8, 2025 at 3:00 PM EST and was removed on November 15, 2025.]
The Duels Machine in Steal a Brainrot uses a duel map with two bases, while the main map has eight. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Duels_Machine | id=A13-0045 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
During Admin Abuse, the New Sammy's Base event in Steal a Brainrot spawns in the middle of the map between two bases, with a staircase leading up to it. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Events | id=A13-0046 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Tung Tung Attack admin event in Steal a Brainrot places a giant Tung Tung Tung Sahur behind the Cave entrance. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Tung_Tung_Tung_Sahur | id=A13-0047 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The page says this is an Admin Abuse exclusive event.]
The Radioactive Event in Steal a Brainrot ran from November 15, 2025 to January 3, 2026 and turned the map into a nuclear reactor outskirts. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Radioactive_Event | id=A13-0048 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-11-15 | checked=2026-11-06]
The Phantom Event in Steal a Brainrot began on June 13, 2026 and turned the map into phantasmal ruins. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Phantom_Event | id=A13-0049 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-06-13 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The infobox lists an end date of July 25, 2026.]
The cave where Brainrots spawn in Steal a Brainrot opens for five minutes every morning at 3:00 AM Eastern Standard Time, according to the Fandom wiki. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/1x1x1x1_Ritual_Event | id=A13-0051 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The page says it is 4:00 AM during Eastern Daylight Time.]
The Shop in Steal a Brainrot, also called the coin shop, is the main place to buy gears, and its items depend on the player's rebirth level. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Shop | id=A13-0052 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]

## Origins and references

Italian brainrot is a series of internet memes that emerged in early 2025, mostly featuring AI-generated animal and object hybrids with Italian-style names. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot | id=A17-0001 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | event=2025 | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://knowyourmeme.com/memes/italian-brainrot-ai-italian-animals | checked=2026-11-06]
Tralalero Tralala is widely considered the first example of the Italian brainrot trend, although the trend's exact origin is unclear. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot | id=A17-0002 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | event=2025-01 | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://knowyourmeme.com/memes/italian-brainrot-ai-italian-animals | checked=2026-11-06]
Internet users made memes of actor Dwayne Johnson rhyming about absurd topics in October 2023, and the nonsense phrase Tralalero tralala from one was later used as the basis for Italian brainrot. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot | id=A17-0003 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2023-10 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Only Wikipedia states this origin link in the pages read, and the Dwayne Johnson memes are not separately sourced there.]
The creation of Tralalero Tralala is often attributed to the TikTok user @eZburger401, who posted the character's audio in January 2025. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot | id=A17-0004 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | event=2025-01 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Wikipedia says the creation is often attributed to this account, which is an attribution rather than a settled fact. Know Your Meme says the account was banned from TikTok in early 2025.] [source: https://knowyourmeme.com/memes/tralalero-tralala | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Tralalero_Tralala | checked=2026-11-06]
Tung Tung Tung Sahur was created in 2025 using DALL-E by the Indonesian TikTok user known online as Noxa. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tung_Tung_Tung_Sahur | id=A17-0006 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | event=2025 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Wikipedia gives the creator's full name; this line uses only the online name. The Steal a Brainrot wiki lists the creator handle as @noxaasht.] [source: https://knowyourmeme.com/memes/tung-tung-tung-sahur | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Tung_Tung_Tung_Sahur | checked=2026-11-06]
The earliest known Tung Tung Tung Sahur image was posted by the TikTok account @noxaasht on February 28, 2025. [source: https://knowyourmeme.com/memes/tung-tung-tung-sahur | id=A17-0007 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | event=2025-02-28 | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tung_Tung_Tung_Sahur | checked=2026-11-06]
The name Tung Tung Tung Sahur comes from an Indonesian saying that alerts Muslims that it is time for sahur, the meal eaten before dawn during Ramadan. [source: https://knowyourmeme.com/memes/tung-tung-tung-sahur | id=A17-0008 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Know Your Meme also says the phrase was used online as early as 2013.] [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tung_Tung_Tung_Sahur | checked=2026-11-06]
Tung Tung Tung Sahur is based on the kentongan, a traditional Indonesian drum. [source: https://knowyourmeme.com/memes/tung-tung-tung-sahur | id=A17-0009 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tung_Tung_Tung_Sahur | checked=2026-11-06]
The name Bombardiro Crocodilo is commonly misspelled as Bombardino Crocodilo or Bombardilo Crocodilo. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot | id=A17-0011 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
In Italian brainrot, Bombardiro Crocodilo is the brother of Bombombini Gusini, a goose with fighter jet wings. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot | id=A17-0012 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The Steal a Brainrot wiki describes Bombombini Gusini's wings differently. See the conflict record for this character.]
The Steal a Brainrot wiki lists @aironicfun and @ncracfbcr4x as the creators of Ballerina Cappuccina. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Ballerina_Cappuccina | id=A17-0014 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki infobox only. The wiki does not give a source for this creator entry.]
The Steal a Brainrot wiki lists @alexey_pigeon as the creator of Chimpanzini Bananini. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Chimpanzini_Bananini | id=A17-0015 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki infobox only. The same handle is listed as creator of many other brainrots on the wiki.]
The Steal a Brainrot wiki lists @mrbrainrot420 as the creator of Trippi Troppi. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Trippi_Troppi | id=A17-0016 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki infobox only.]
The original Trippi Troppi is a fish-bear, and the cat-shrimp version is the more popular and iconic one, according to the Steal a Brainrot wiki. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Trippi_Troppi | id=A17-0017 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot | checked=2026-11-06]
The Steal a Brainrot wiki lists ofuscabreno as the creator of Brr Brr Patapim. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Brr_Brr_Patapim | id=A17-0018 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki infobox only.]
Boneca Ambalabu is one of the Indonesian spin-offs of the Italian brainrot trend, alongside Tung Tung Tung Sahur and Hotspot Bro. [source: https://knowyourmeme.com/memes/italian-brainrot-ai-italian-animals | id=A17-0019 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Single source. The Steal a Brainrot wiki gives its creator as @ofuscabreno, which was not checked further.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Boneca_Ambalabu | checked=2026-11-06]
The Steal a Brainrot wiki lists mzic2403 as the creator of Strawberry Elephant. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Strawberry_Elephant | id=A17-0020 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki infobox only.]
The Steal a Brainrot wiki lists zhumaokele as the creator of Meowl. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Meowl | id=A17-0021 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki infobox only.]
John Pork is a viral internet meme character with a human body and the head of a pig. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/John_Pork | id=A17-0022 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Steal a Brainrot wiki says John Pork is a fictional character and not a real person. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/John_Pork | id=A17-0023 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Nyan Cat is a YouTube video uploaded in April 2011 that became an internet meme. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nyan_Cat | id=A17-0024 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | event=2011-04 | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Gattatino_Nyanino | checked=2026-11-06]
The Nyan Cat GIF was posted on April 2, 2011 by Christopher Torres on his LOL-Comics website. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nyan_Cat | id=A17-0025 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2011-04-02 | checked=2026-11-06]
The original Nyan Cat song, Nyanyanyanyanyanyanya!, was first uploaded to Niconico by the user daniwell on July 25, 2010. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nyan_Cat | id=A17-0026 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2010-07-25 | checked=2026-11-06]
The 6-7 meme was further popularized in March 2025 by a boy who became known as the 67 Kid. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/6-7_(meme) | id=A17-0029 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | event=2025-03 | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/67 | checked=2026-11-06]
The 67 brainrot in Steal a Brainrot is based on the 67 meme, which features a blonde boy known as the 67 Kid shouting six-seven. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/67 | id=A17-0030 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/6-7_(meme) | checked=2026-11-06]
Skibidi Toilet is an animated web series by Alexey Gerasimov that first aired on YouTube in February 2023. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Skibidi_Toilet | id=A17-0032 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | event=2023-02-07 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The February 7, 2023 start date is from the Wikipedia infobox.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Skibidi_Toilet | checked=2026-11-06]
Skibidi Toilet is the first and only human-type Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot that originally comes from a meme. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Human-Based_Brainrots | id=A17-0033 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Skibidi_Toilet | checked=2026-11-06]
Appelini in Steal a Brainrot is based on AI Fruits, a TikTok series about humanoid fruit. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Appelini | id=A17-0034 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Bananito in Steal a Brainrot was inspired by AI Fruits, a series on TikTok. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bananito | id=A17-0035 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Strawberrita in Steal a Brainrot was inspired by an AI-generated TikTok series called Fruit Love Island, also known as AI Fruits. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Strawberrita | id=A17-0036 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The NPC version of Bambu Bambu Sahur on the Aura Farm Boat is inspired by the Indonesian Aura kid meme. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bambu_Bambu_Sahur | id=A17-0037 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Boatito Auratito in Steal a Brainrot is a reference to the Aura Farming Kid meme. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Boatito_Auratito | id=A17-0038 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Dul Dul Dul is an Indonesian brainrot based on the hairstyle gundul, which means bald. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Dul_Dul_Dul | id=A17-0039 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Horegini Boom in Steal a Brainrot is based on a culture in Indonesia called Sound Horeg. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Horegini_Boom | id=A17-0040 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
La Cucaracha in Steal a Brainrot is inspired by the Mexican song La Cucaracha. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/La_Cucaracha | id=A17-0041 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Mi Gatito in Steal a Brainrot is inspired by the song Ay Mi Gatito by Princesita Kelly. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Mi_Gatito | id=A17-0042 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The same wiki page's infobox names Princesita Kelly as creator.]
Chicleteira Champeona in Steal a Brainrot has a red shirt and pants with the number 19 as a reference to the footballer Lamine Yamal. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Chicleteira_Champeona | id=A17-0043 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The name of Capitano Americano in Steal a Brainrot is based on Captain America. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Capitano_Americano | id=A17-0044 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Chicken Zombie in Steal a Brainrot is a reference to Chicken Jockey from Minecraft. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Chicken_Zombie | id=A17-0045 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Clickerino Crabo was inspired by a deleted Roblox game called Crab Clicker. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Clickerino_Crabo | id=A17-0046 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Donkeyturbo Express in Steal a Brainrot is a reference to the Brainrot Express of the North Pole Event. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Donkeyturbo_Express | id=A17-0047 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The page also says both are based on The Polar Express, a Christmas movie. That part was not recorded as a fact.]
The design of Elefanto Frigo in Steal a Brainrot is inspired by the riddle of how to put an elephant in a fridge. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Elefanto_Frigo | id=A17-0048 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Venuspino's design in Steal a Brainrot is clearly inspired by the Piranha Plant from the Mario series. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Venuspino | id=A17-0050 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Draculino in Steal a Brainrot is based on Dracula. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Draculino | id=A17-0051 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Headless Horseman in Steal a Brainrot is based on the short story The Legend of Sleepy Hollow, written by Washington Irving in 1820. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Headless_Horseman | id=A17-0052 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | event=1820 | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Legend_of_Sleepy_Hollow | checked=2026-11-06]
1x1x1x1 in Steal a Brainrot is based on the Roblox myth of the same name. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/1x1x1x1 | id=A17-0053 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Guest 666 in Steal a Brainrot is based on the Roblox myth of the same name. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Guest_666 | id=A17-0054 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
John Doe in Steal a Brainrot is based on a famous Roblox myth. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/John_Doe | id=A17-0055 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The music video SIGMA BOY x BRAINROT EXTINCTION, released on 22 May 2025, inspired the Extinct Event in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Extinct_Matteo | id=A17-0056 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-05-22 | checked=2026-11-06]
The Escape Tsunami LTM in Steal a Brainrot is based on another popular Roblox game, Escape Tsunami For Brainrots. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Escape_Tsunami_LTM | id=A17-0057 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
In Steal a Brainrot, Recognized Brainrots are brainrots that the community widely considers to have an elevated value compared to others of the same rarity. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Brainrots | id=A17-0062 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=This is a wiki glossary definition, not a list of which brainrots qualify.]
Some Steal a Brainrot characters have no real-world origin image because Sammy created them as original made-up brainrots, and their images are fanmade. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Origin_Images | id=A17-0063 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki marks fanmade origin images with an asterisk.]
Brain rot was named Oxford Word of the Year in 2024. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot | id=A17-0064 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2024 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Wikipedia cites Oxford University Press for this. The OUP page itself was not read.]
Tung Tung Tung Sahur and Ballerina Cappuccina skins were added to Fortnite Battle Royale in April 2026. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot | id=A17-0066 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-04 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Wikipedia says some fans criticized these skins before release. Not verified against Fortnite's own pages.]
Bear Family Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot are based on bears, and the first one, Trippi Troppi Troppa Trippa, was made by TikTok user @1raidex_. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bear_Family | id=A17-0067 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Community wiki only. The subject is the family page and is not the Headless Horseman.]

## Collaborations and crossovers

The Bruno Mars concert in Steal a Brainrot was a one-day event on January 17, 2026, which Roblox promoted on X the day before. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/steal-a-brainrots-bruno-mars-concert-hits-12-7-million-concurrent-players-3305913/ | id=A18-0001 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | pub=2026-01-17 | event=2026-01-17 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Dexerto quotes Roblox's X post of January 16, 2026. Guinness World Records gives the same date.] [source: https://www.guinnessworldrecords.com/news/commercial/2026/2/bruno-mars-hosts-largest-concert-in-a-videogame-during-steal-a-brainrot-on-roblox | checked=2026-11-06]
Guinness World Records lists the Steal a Brainrot Bruno Mars concert as the largest music concert in a videogame by a single artist. [source: https://www.guinnessworldrecords.com/world-records/782027-largest-music-concert-in-a-videogame-by-a-single-artist | id=A18-0003 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-01-17 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Guinness lists the record under Steal a Brainrot, Roblox and Bruno Mars.] [source: https://www.guinnessworldrecords.com/news/commercial/2026/2/bruno-mars-hosts-largest-concert-in-a-videogame-during-steal-a-brainrot-on-roblox | checked=2026-11-06]
Think Influence became the exclusive global agent for Steal a Brainrot in September 2025, to expand the game into consumer products, partnerships and media. [source: https://www.licenseglobal.com/video-games/think-influence-appointed-exclusive-global-agent-for-steal-a-brainrot- | id=A18-0005 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | pub=2025-09-18 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Think Influence is also named as a producing partner on the Steal a Brainrot movie.]
Fortnite's Steal the Brainrot Creative map is an officially licensed Steal a Brainrot experience, and it is not the Stealing Brainrots map that Spyder Games sued in 2025. [source: https://www.pockettactics.com/fortnite/steal-the-brainrot-lawsuit | id=A18-0006 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | pub=2025-10-25 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Pocket Tactics quotes the lawyer's filing. The Fandom wiki describes the Fortnite version's origin differently, naming a creator called ferins and a Razer collaboration, so the origin is not settled.]
Jandel, the creator of Grow a Garden, challenged Sammy to an Admin Abuse war on X in August 2025, offering to donate $5,000 to Team Water for every one million players Grow a Garden had more than Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://techwiser.com/grow-a-garden-steal-a-brainrot-admin-abuse-war/ | id=A18-0007 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2025-08-15 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The challenge was a post on X by Jandel. The war itself was held on August 23, 2025.]
Steakini Fattini is the first Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot based on a streamer, and it is largely based on YouTuber Steak's Roblox avatar. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Steakini_Fattini | id=A18-0008 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-05-30 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Fandom wiki only. The Steak Admin Abuse of May 30, 2026 was not found in press search.]
Caylusaurus is the second Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot based on a streamer, after Steakini Fattini, and it is based on YouTuber Caylus. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Caylusaurus | id=A18-0009 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-06-06 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Fandom wiki only. The Caylus Admin Abuse of June 6, 2026 was not found in press search.]
The songs played in the Steal a Brainrot Bruno Mars concert were I Just Might and Locked Out of Heaven. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bruno_Mars_Event | id=A18-0011 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-01-17 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Fandom wiki only. Dexerto says the concert featured several of Bruno Mars's songs.]
Brunito Marsito is the first official celebrity collaboration Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Brunito_Marsito | id=A18-0012 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-01-17 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Fandom wiki's own claim. Press search did not check it independently.]
John Pork was added to Steal a Brainrot after the meme's creator gave permission for the character to be used in the game. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/John_Pork | id=A18-0014 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-05-02 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Fandom wiki only. The OG page gives the same reason. Released May 2, 2026 per the wiki.]
Steal a Brainrot has an exclusive Rosey and Teddy reskin for YouTuber Nachito, which adds a black tuxedo to Teddy when the Matteo Hat trait is applied. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rosey_and_Teddy | id=A18-0015 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Fandom wiki only.]

## Community and culture

Sammy (SpyderSammy) said in an X post that Sammy would match Jandel's wager and donate to Team Water no matter who won the Admin Abuse War. [source: https://beebom.com/poll-who-will-win-admin-abuse-war-grow-a-garden-vs-steal-a-brainrot/ | id=A06-0003 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | pub=2025-08-23 | event=2025-08 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Sammy's own post was not opened. Beebom quotes it.]
Steal a Brainrot won the Best Creative Direction award at the 2025 Roblox Innovation Awards, which were announced in September 2025. [source: https://www.pocketgamer.biz/roblox-reveals-2025-innovation-awards-winners | id=A06-0007 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | pub=2025-09-09 | event=2025-09 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Grow a Garden won the most awards that year.] [source: https://beebom.com/roblox-innovation-awards-2025-winners | checked=2026-11-06]
A TikTok clip of a player screaming after one of their Brainrots was stolen in Steal a Brainrot was posted on June 15, 2025, and gained over 800,000 views in nine days. [source: https://knowyourmeme.com/memes/subcultures/steal-a-brainrot | id=A06-0020 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | event=2025-06-15 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=View count as reported by Know Your Meme. Wikipedia reports that children crying over stolen Brainrots drew viral TikTok and YouTube videos.] [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot | checked=2026-11-06]
The removal of Tung Tung Tung Sahur from Steal a Brainrot drew online backlash and memes in mid-September 2025, with some people blaming Sammy and others blaming Noxa and Mementum Lab. [source: https://knowyourmeme.com/memes/events/tung-tung-tung-sahur-removed-from-steal-a-brainrot | id=A06-0021 | type=community | status=corroborated | confidence=high | pub=2025-09-17 | event=2025-09 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Dataconomy also reports widespread backlash and misinformation circulating after the removal.] [source: https://dataconomy.com/2025/09/19/steal-a-brainrot-removes-tung-tung-tung-sahur-character-over-copyright | checked=2026-11-06]
A 2017 rumor said the John Doe Roblox account would hack players every March 18, and Steal a Brainrot's John Doe Hacking event ran on March 18, 2026. [source: https://techwiser.com/steal-a-brainrot-john-doe/ | id=A06-0025 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-03-18 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The rumor part is from TechWiser. The 2026 event date is from the wiki only. The wiki says the event was most likely staged, and no reputable press was found confirming a real intrusion.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/John_Doe_Hacking | checked=2026-11-06]
Steal a Brainrot's developer Sammy has many impersonators on YouTube, according to the community wiki, and Sammy's main personal channel is mostly used to talk to YouTubers in streams. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Socials | id=A06-0027 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Wiki claim, not checked against YouTube. The Star reports lawsuits against alleged imposters of the game, which is a different matter.]

## Merchandise, legal and media

PhatMojo announced in February 2026 that it had signed global toy rights to Steal a Brainrot, with the toy launch planned for Q3 2026. [source: https://www.mojo-nation.com/phatmojo-to-launch-steal-a-brainrot-toys-later-this-year/ | id=A15-0001 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | pub=2026-02-06 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Trade article only. The article says product details and categories will be announced later. The signing date is not stated.]
The Steal a Brainrot toy deal with PhatMojo was brokered by the licensing agency Think Influence. [source: https://www.mojo-nation.com/phatmojo-to-launch-steal-a-brainrot-toys-later-this-year/ | id=A15-0002 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | pub=2026-02-06 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Trade article only. Think Influence is also named in the Story Kitchen movie announcement.]
Steal a Brainrot blind box figures come with a DLC code, according to Hot Topic and ToyWiz listings. [source: https://www.hottopic.com/product/steal-a-brainrot-blind-box-figure/36355854.html | id=A15-0003 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Retailer product listings. The codes unlock items in the Roblox game. The Hot Topic listing describes a blind box figure, and the ToyWiz listing describes a mystery pack with one random figure.] [source: https://toywiz.com/roblox-steal-a-brainrot-mini-figures-mystery-pack-includes-dlc-code-/ | checked=2026-11-06]
As of November 2026, ToyWiz sold a Steal a Brainrot mini figure mystery pack with one random figure and a DLC code for $9.99. [source: https://toywiz.com/roblox-steal-a-brainrot-mini-figures-mystery-pack-includes-dlc-code-/ | id=A15-0004 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Retailer listing read on 2026-11-06 showing the item as in stock. The listing is undated, and the price is the listed US price for one pack.]
As of November 2026, Hot Topic listed the Steal A Brainrot Blind Box Figure at $9.90, but it was out of stock. [source: https://www.hottopic.com/product/steal-a-brainrot-blind-box-figure/36355854.html | id=A15-0005 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Retailer listing read on 2026-11-06. The listing is undated and shows a 3.4 out of 5 customer rating.]
Steal a Brainrot plushies sold on playsab.com come with a code, and redeeming that code in the game gives the matching Brainrot, which appears in the player's base. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Brainrot_Plushies | id=A15-0009 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Fandom wiki only. Playsab.com page also says its products come with a redeemable code.] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Playsab.com | checked=2026-11-06]
The Boppin Bunny plushie is the first plush Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot that can receive mutations when bought. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Boppin_Bunny | id=A15-0011 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Fandom wiki trivia only. Festive 67 cannot receive mutations when bought.]
The Fandom MerchShop DLC page lists in-game rewards for merch codes, including 2x Server Luck for 10 minutes and specific Brainrots such as Polaroidini. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/MerchShop_DLC | id=A15-0014 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Fandom wiki table only, not checked in-game. The table lists reward tiers by DLC name. Code strings are deliberately left out.]
The Fandom wiki's Brainrot Plushies gallery lists Steal a Brainrot Series 1 figures sold as Mystery Toys, such as 67, Tralalero Tralala and Ballerina Cappuccina. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Brainrot_Plushies | id=A15-0015 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The gallery as read has Series 1 sections only and no Series 2 section. No Series 2 minifigure source was found in press or retailer searches.]
The Fortnite Stealing Brainrots lawsuit asks for the map to be taken down, along with damages and profits, according to press coverage of the complaint. [source: https://insider-gaming.com/steal-a-brainrot-developers-sue-fortnite-copycat/ | id=A15-0018 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | pub=2025-10-24 | event=2025-10 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The relief sought is what the plaintiffs asked for in the complaint. It is not a court order. The complaint is reported to seek a jury trial, damages and profits.] [source: https://www.pockettactics.com/fortnite/steal-the-brainrot-lawsuit | checked=2026-11-06]
Adam Starr is the attorney representing Spyder Games and Speedy Simulator Gaming in the lawsuit over the Fortnite Stealing Brainrots map. [source: https://insider-gaming.com/steal-a-brainrot-developers-sue-fortnite-copycat/ | id=A15-0019 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | pub=2025-10-24 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Adam Starr is also named as general counsel at Do Big Studios by GamesBeat in February 2026.] [source: https://www.pockettactics.com/fortnite/steal-the-brainrot-lawsuit | checked=2026-11-06]
Steal a Brainrot is registered with the US Copyright Office, according to Wikipedia. [source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot | id=A15-0020 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Wikipedia cites the registration number. The number is left out here. The plaintiffs' lawyer also says the companies acquired a copyright in August, with no year stated in the article.]
Mementum Lab issued a covenant not to sue Spyder Games and Speedy Simulator Gaming over the original listed characters in Steal a Brainrot, and argued this makes the copyright dispute moot. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/tung-tung-sahur-is-at-the-center-of-a-bizarre-federal-custody-battle-over-brainrot-characters-3381733/ | id=A15-0021 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | pub=2026-07-01 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Mementum's argument as reported by Dexerto. The Steal a Brainrot side disputes that it solves the issue.]
In a June 24, 2026 filing, Spyder Games and Speedy Simulator Gaming argued that Mementum Lab's covenant not to sue is too narrow for the dispute. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/tung-tung-sahur-is-at-the-center-of-a-bizarre-federal-custody-battle-over-brainrot-characters-3381733/ | id=A15-0022 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | pub=2026-07-01 | event=2026-06-24 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Year is inferred from the July 2026 article date. Argument in a filing, not a ruling.]
Spyder Games and Speedy Simulator Gaming argued in a motion that Tung Tung Sahur is a fictional character, not a trademark source identifier, and that Mementum Lab is using trademark law to control creative content. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/tung-tung-sahur-is-at-the-center-of-a-bizarre-federal-custody-battle-over-brainrot-characters-3381733/ | id=A15-0023 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | pub=2026-07-01 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=A party's argument in a motion to dismiss Mementum's amended counterclaims, as reported by Dexerto. Not a ruling. The motion date is not stated.]
Mementum Lab says in court filings that it has already licensed Tung Tung Sahur for other games and products, including Fortnite and toys. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/tung-tung-sahur-is-at-the-center-of-a-bizarre-federal-custody-battle-over-brainrot-characters-3381733/ | id=A15-0024 | type=secondary | status=unverified | confidence=medium | pub=2026-07-01 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Mementum's own claim, as reported by Dexerto. Not checked against the filings and not a court finding.]

## Security and moderation

In Steal a Brainrot, Sammy deleted all the Headless Horsemen that were spawned during the October 22, 2026 hack, according to the community wiki. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/John_Doe_Hacking | id=A14-0001 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | event=2026-10-22 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Only the community Fandom wiki was found for this incident. Searches of news sites turned up no independent report, and the Fandom forum page could not be opened (HTTP 403).]
Tung Tung Tung Sahur was removed from Steal a Brainrot on September 13, 2025, after the developer Sammy pulled the character during licensing talks with the creator's agency. [source: https://techwiser.com/why-tung-tung-tung-sahur-removed-steal-a-brainrot/ | id=A14-0003 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=high | pub=2025-09-17 | event=2025-09-13 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The account of the removal comes from the creator's agency, which said the creator did not request it. The developers said in a TikTok statement that they were always open to discussion. Dataconomy reports the removal as] [source: https://dataconomy.com/2025/09/19/steal-a-brainrot-removes-tung-tung-tung-sahur-character-over-copyright | checked=2026-11-06] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Tung_Tung_Tung_Sahur | checked=2026-11-06]
The Brainrot Dealer machine in Steal a Brainrot was removed on November 15, 2025, one week after it launched on November 8, 2025, after the developer Sammy listened to player feedback. [source: https://techwiser.com/steal-a-brainrot-dealer-how-use-guide/ | id=A14-0004 | type=secondary | status=corroborated | confidence=medium | pub=2025-11-09 | event=2025-11-15 | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki says the Dealer was replaced by the Brainrot Trader and cites complaints about its fairness. The TechWiser guide was published two days after launch, so its removal date was a scheduled date and not a report of ] [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Brainrot_Dealer | checked=2026-11-06]

## Secrets, trivia and sounds

Spyder Elephant is the only Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot that gets a trait instantly when obtained, the Spider trait. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Spyder_Elephant | id=A19-0003 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Spyder Elephant is the only OG in Steal a Brainrot that does not appear in the Index. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Spyder_Elephant | id=A19-0004 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Spyder Elephant is the first reskin of an OG in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Spyder_Elephant | id=A19-0005 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki says it reskins Strawberry Elephant, the first OG.]
Spyder Elephant shares the Strawberry Event and its sound effects with Strawberry Elephant in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Spyder_Elephant | id=A19-0006 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Only 25 Spyder Chains were on sale in Steal a Brainrot, so only 25 Spyder Elephants were meant to exist. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Spyder_Elephant | id=A19-0007 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=Wiki claim. The same page says a leaked exist count was 14 at one point.]
Bloodmoon was the first seasonal event in Steal a Brainrot to introduce a mutation that could only be obtained during its limited time window. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bloodmoon_Event | id=A19-0008 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
67 is the first typographic or numerical Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/67 | id=A19-0009 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
1x1x1x1 is alphabetically the first Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/1x1x1x1 | id=A19-0010 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Bombardiro Crocodilo is the only Mythic Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot that has a ritual to spawn its multiple versions, Los Crocodilitos. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bombardiro_Crocodilo | id=A19-0011 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Coffin Tung Tung Tung Sahur is the first lucky block in Steal a Brainrot with an animation. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Coffin_Tung_Tung_Tung_Sahur | id=A19-0012 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Coffin Tung Tung Tung Sahur is the first Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot that can crawl, as its walk animation. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Coffin_Tung_Tung_Tung_Sahur | id=A19-0013 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The sound that Coffin Tung Tung Tung Sahur plays in Steal a Brainrot is the song Astronomia by Vicetone and Tony igy. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Coffin_Tung_Tung_Tung_Sahur | id=A19-0014 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki says it is the same song used in the Coffin Dance meme.]
Scarino Crowino is the first scarecrow Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Scarino_Crowino | id=A19-0015 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Candini Fluffini is the first cotton candy Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Candini_Fluffini | id=A19-0016 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Harpuccino is the first Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot that is a musical instrument, excluding Cavallo Virtuoso. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Harpuccino | id=A19-0017 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The wiki excludes Cavallo Virtuoso because it is a horse fused with a piano.]
Harpuccino and Berenjello Angello are the only two Mythic Brainrots in Steal a Brainrot that were obtainable from the Divine Fuse. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Harpuccino | id=A19-0018 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Orcaledon has six crafting recipes in the Brainrot Trader in Steal a Brainrot, the most of any Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Orcaledon | id=A19-0019 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
When the Turkey Event activates in Steal a Brainrot, a text pops up saying "A Turkey has spawned on the map!" [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Turkey_Event | id=A19-0020 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Gobblino Uniciclino must be hit 10 times in the Turkey Event before it walks to the player's base in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Turkey_Event | id=A19-0021 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Gobblino Uniciclino is the only Brainrot in Steal a Brainrot that can spawn off the track rather than from a Fuse Machine. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Turkey_Event | id=A19-0022 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Steal a Brainrot wiki says the Bruno Mars event is actually called Lance, though many players call it the Bruno Event. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bruno_Mars_Event | id=A19-0023 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Bruno Mars Lance event in Steal a Brainrot was activated only once and lasted 7 minutes and 30 seconds. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bruno_Mars_Event | id=A19-0024 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The 7 minutes 30 seconds length is a separate line on the same page.]
The songs played in the Bruno Mars Lance event in Steal a Brainrot were I Just Might and Locked Out of Heaven. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Bruno_Mars_Event | id=A19-0025 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Ominous Dromen is the first soundtrack a player hears during gameplay in Steal a Brainrot, with day and night variants. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Ambient | id=A19-0028 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Ominous Dromen in Steal a Brainrot is a remix and extension of Ominous Dream by SkittlegirlSound. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Ambient | id=A19-0029 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Ominous Dromen (Intense) is the main soundtrack of the Los Crocodillitos event in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Rituals | id=A19-0030 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Anniversary Celebration Mix, the main soundtrack of the 1 Year event in Steal a Brainrot, mashes up four songs of one minute each. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Admin | id=A19-0031 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The four songs are Raining Tacos, Crab Rave, PASSO BEM SOLTO and La Cucaracha.]
Crab Rave, the main soundtrack of the crab event in Steal a Brainrot, was made by Noisestorm under the Monstercat release. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Admin | id=A19-0032 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Raining Tacos, the main soundtrack of the raining tacos event in Steal a Brainrot, was made by Parry Gripp. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Admin | id=A19-0033 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Tung Tung Tung Sahur (Italian Brainrot) is the main soundtrack of the concert event in Steal a Brainrot and was made by W&W. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Admin | id=A19-0034 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Brainrot Rap is the main soundtrack of the rap concert event in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Admin | id=A19-0035 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Bazooka is the main soundtrack of Granny's Funeral event in Steal a Brainrot and was made by Miami XO. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Admin | id=A19-0036 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Alien's UFO is the main soundtrack of the alien invasion event in Steal a Brainrot and is heard only when that event activates. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Admin | id=A19-0037 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Holly Jolly Christmas is the main soundtrack of the North Pole event in Steal a Brainrot and is heard when the player takes the Brainrot Express train. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Seasonal | id=A19-0038 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Indonesia event in Steal a Brainrot has two soundtracks, one being Young, Black & Rich by Melly Mike. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Country | id=A19-0039 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06 | note=The in-game version has swearing removed. La Cucaracha is the main soundtrack of the Mexican event in Steal a Brainrot, with its song made by Mariachi Peralta. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Country | id=A19-0041 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Bloodmoon Creepy Ambience is the main soundtrack of the Bloodmoon event in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Legacy | id=A19-0042 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Rain is the main soundtrack of the rain weather event in Steal a Brainrot, and it can be heard naturally because rain spawns randomly. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Weather | id=A19-0043 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Witching Hour is the main soundtrack of the Witching Hour event in Steal a Brainrot and was also used for the Grave Digger event. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Soundtracks/Seasonal | id=A19-0045 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The "Someone is stealing your..." sound in Steal a Brainrot plays from the point of view of the player being stolen from. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Sound_Effects | id=A19-0046 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Taco Explosion is a super-charged explosion in Steal a Brainrot that happens when a Taco brainrot, Taco Truck Brainrot, Taco Lucky Block or Los Taco Blocks spawns. The Golden Explosion in Steal a Brainrot happens when a Gold Elf spawns. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Sound_Effects/Spawns | id=A19-0048 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Nyan Explosion in Steal a Brainrot is a rainbow explosion that happens when Gattatino Nyanino spawns. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Sound_Effects/Spawns | id=A19-0049 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Strawberry Explosion in Steal a Brainrot is a strawberry-colored explosion that happens when a Strawberry Elephant spawns. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Sound_Effects/Spawns | id=A19-0050 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Easter Index in Steal a Brainrot contained 13 brainrots, of which 10 were required for the Easter Base. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Easter_Index | id=A19-0052 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Easter Hour is the first seasonal hour event in Steal a Brainrot in which all the Brainrots that can spawn are Secret. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Easter_Hour | id=A19-0053 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Noo my Eggs was the rarest Brainrot to get out of the Easter Hour in Steal a Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Noo_my_Eggs | id=A19-0054 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]

The Dul Dul Dul ritual in Steal a Brainrot is the first ritual that does not spawn a baby Brainrot. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rituals | id=A19-0056 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Karkerkar Kurkur ritual in Steal a Brainrot is the first ritual that spawns no event or mutation. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rituals | id=A19-0057 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
The Chicleteira Bicicleteira, Mi Gatito and Money Money Puggy rituals in Steal a Brainrot each require only 2 specific Brainrots. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Rituals | id=A19-0059 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Rainbow and Gold appeared in the one-year anniversary leak in Steal a Brainrot, but Sammy never released it. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Removed_Content | id=A19-0065 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Flappin Clover was leaked as the new DLC Brainrot for Easter in Steal a Brainrot, and then changed to Boppin Bunny. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Removed_Content | id=A19-0066 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]
Coffin Tung Tung Tung Sahur is a lucky block that spawns only during Admin Abuse in Steal a Brainrot and opens to Tung Tung Tung Sahur. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Removed_Content | id=A19-0067 | type=community | status=unverified | confidence=medium | checked=2026-11-06]

# Conflict - all-time peak in October 2025: PocketGamer.biz says 25.4 million [source: https://www.pocketgamer.biz/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-becomes-first-game-to-surpass-25m-concurrent-players/]; Outlook Respawn says 25.2 million [source: https://respawn.outlookindia.com/gaming/gaming-news/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-breaks-all-time-player-record]; Story Kitchen says 25.8 million [source: https://www.story-kitchen.com/news/story-kitchen-partnering-with-steal-a-brainrot-creators-on-feature-adaptation-of-roblox-game]; Dexerto repeats 25.8 million [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/robloxs-steal-a-brainrot-is-being-adapted-into-a-movie-3311810/]; the Fandom wiki says 25,836,222 at Update 20 on October 11, 2025, which is after PocketGamer.biz's October 7 report [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Steal_a_Brainrot_(Game)]

Side B: Sportskeeda October 2026 lists 'Lucky' under Lucky Pot at 5x. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Lucky_Pot | id=A10-0071 | checked=2026-11-06]

# Conflict - Weather trait names: The trait names given by the weather events are disputed: Sportskeeda's August 2025 article calls them Rain, Snow and Starshine, while the Fandom Events page calls them Wet, Snowy and Cometstruck. Side A: Sportskeeda (this URL) gives Rain trait, Snow trait and Starshine trait. Side B: Fandom Events page says 'When this event is active, it makes it so brainrots have a chance to get the Wet trait.' for Rain, Snowy for Snow, and Cometstruck for Starfall. [source: https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-fuse-machine-update-patch-notes | id=A10-0073 | checked=2026-11-06]
# Conflict - Weather events: The Fandom Update 4 page lists weather events as added in Update 4, which conflicts with the Update 10 page and Sportskeeda, which date the weather events to Update 10 (August 1, 2025). Side A: Update 4 page (this URL). Side B: Update 10 page lists 'NEW WEATHER EVENTS' and Sportskeeda dates them to August 1, 2025. The Update 4 page's other content (Rainbow mutation, June 2025) matches the DB update history, so the weather line looks like a later addition or error. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_04 | id=A10-0076 | checked=2026-11-06]

Beebom guide (Nov 27, 2025): $15M cash. https://www.sportskeeda.com/roblox-news/steal-brainrot-lucky-blocks-all-lucky-blocks-price-list and https://beebom.com/steal-a-brainrot-lucky-blocks-guide [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Brainrot_God_Lucky_Block | id=A11-0003 | checked=2026-11-06]

Sportskeeda price list (modified Sep 30, 2025): 2,999 Robux. Cash price of 750M is consistent across sources. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log | id=A12-0015 | checked=2026-11-06]
