The Update 59 log (https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Update_Log/Update_59) lists the Crystal Event as new but gives no date. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Crystal_Event | id=A13-0050 | checked=2026-11-06]
# Conflict - Boppin Bunny plushie: The Fandom wiki gives two different initial stock numbers for the Boppin Bunny plushie: 30,000 on the MerchShop DLC page and 50,000 on the Playsab.com page. MerchShop DLC page: Boppin Bunny initial stock 30,000. Playsab.com page: Boppin Bunny had 50,000 stock. Festive 67 is 50,000 on both pages. Neither figure is confirmed by the store. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/MerchShop_DLC | id=A15-0008 | checked=2026-11-06]
# Conflict - Festive 67 release date: The Fandom wiki gives two different dates for when Festive 67 was added to Steal a Brainrot: December 13, 2025 on its own page and November 13, 2025 on the Limited Quantity Brainrots page. Festive 67 page: 13/12/25. Limited Quantity Brainrots page: 13/11/25. The wiki Codes page says codes were added on December 13, 2025, which fits the Festive 67 page. Dates are written day/month/year on the wiki. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Festive_67 | id=A15-0013 | checked=2026-11-06]
# Conflict - Steal a Brainrot: Sources disagree on how many concurrent players Steal a Brainrot had on August 23, 2025: PocketGamer.biz, citing Offpath data, says 20 million, while IGN, citing Fortnite.gg data, says 15 million. PocketGamer.biz (2025-08-27) relays a LinkedIn post by Roblox strategy consultant James Purell that uses Offpath data, giving 20 million for Steal a Brainrot and 21.1 million for Grow a Garden. Dexerto (2025-08-25) repeats the IGN numbers. Wikipedia says 20 million and cites IGN, but the IGN text gives 15 million. Roblox's letter gives no per-game figure. Neither number is official. [source: https://www.ign.com/articles/roblox-hits-fresh-player-record-towering-over-steam-and-fortnite-amid-war-between-its-two-most-popular-games-grow-a-garden-and-steal-a-brainrot | id=A16-0005 | checked=2026-11-06]
# Conflict - Roblox: Tracker figures for Roblox's platform peak on August 23, 2025 differ: Dexerto says 47.3 million and PocketGamer.biz says 47.4 million, while Roblox's own letter says over 45 million. Dexerto's 47.3 million matches the Fortnite.gg-based figure in IGN (2025-08-26), which says 47 million. PocketGamer's 47.4 million relays the Offpath-based post. Wikipedia uses 47.4 million and cites PocketGamer.biz. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/roblox-smashes-concurrent-user-record-thanks-to-war-between-two-games-3242035/ | id=A16-0006 | checked=2026-11-06]
# Conflict - Steal a Brainrot: Sources disagree on Steal a Brainrot's September 2025 peak: Dexerto says the Roblox version reached 23.4 million, its 24 million headline adds the Fortnite version, and Wikipedia and PocketGamer.biz give 24 million and 24.1 million. Dexerto dates the 23.4 million figure to September 14 in its text, but the embedded post by community account ShiinaBR is dated September 13, 2025. Dexerto's 542,000 is from the separate Fortnite Creative version, so do not read 24 million as a Roblox-only figure. Wikipedia dates its 24 million to September 13 and cites Gaming News, which I could not open. PocketGamer.biz's 24.1 million has no source given. [source: https://www.dexerto.com/gaming/steal-a-brainrot-smashes-records-with-nearly-24-million-players-across-roblox-and-fortnite-3250973/ | id=A16-0007 | checked=2026-11-06]
# Conflict - Grow a Garden: Sources disagree on Grow a Garden's peak on August 23, 2025: IGN, citing Fortnite.gg data, says 22 million; Wikipedia says at least 22.3 million; PocketGamer.biz, citing Offpath data, says 21.1 million. Roblox's letter says Grow a Garden set a record of nearly 22 million in July 2025, and PocketGamer.biz gives 21.3 million for June. The dates and numbers differ across these sources. [source: https://www.ign.com/articles/roblox-hits-fresh-player-record-towering-over-steam-and-fortnite-amid-war-between-its-two-most-popular-games-grow-a-garden-and-steal-a-brainrot | id=A16-0026 | checked=2026-11-06]
# Conflict - Tralalero Tralala: Sources disagree on the exact debut date of Tralalero Tralala: the Steal a Brainrot wiki says January 13, 2025, while Know Your Meme says the sound was in use from early January 2025. Wiki side: January 13, 2025 (https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Tralalero_Tralala). Know Your Meme side: the original sound was used in videos from January 8 and January 10, and the post was sometime in early January 2025 (https://knowyourmeme.com/memes/tralalero-tralala). Wikipedia (https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_brainrot) names a different 13 January 2025 post by another user. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Tralalero_Tralala | id=A17-0005 | checked=2026-11-06]
# Conflict - Bruno Mars concert viewer count: Sources disagree on the audience for the Steal a Brainrot Bruno Mars concert: Guinness World Records gives 12,862,161 concurrent viewers, while Dexerto gives a daily peak of 12,712,359 players. Dexerto: https://www.dexerto.com/roblox/steal-a-brainrots-bruno-mars-concert-hits-12-7-million-concurrent-players-3305913/ . The two figures measure different things: a concurrent-viewer count versus a daily peak. Wikipedia gives 12.8 million. [source: https://www.guinnessworldrecords.com/world-records/782027-largest-music-concert-in-a-videogame-by-a-single-artist | id=A18-0002 | checked=2026-11-06]
# Conflict - Survive LAVA for Brainrots owner: Sources disagree on who owns the Roblox game Survive LAVA for Brainrots: the Fandom wiki names Caylus and Steak, while Rotrends lists the studio as Johnny Lava. Rotrends (https://www.rotrends.com/game/9649298941) lists the studio as Johnny Lava and a creation date of January 30, 2026. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Caylusaurus | id=A18-0017 | checked=2026-11-06]
# Conflict - Polaroidini: The number of Polaroidini in Steal a Brainrot is listed as 24 on the Fandom wiki infobox, while Sammy's confirmed answer POLAROIDINI23 gives 23 at the time of the recording. The Sammy confirmed line says 23 of it exist, from the November 5, 2026 recording. The confirmed line is left unchanged. The wiki's count could be newer or wrong. [source: https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/wiki/Polaroidini | id=A20-0013 | checked=2026-11-06]
# Conflict - date of Jandel's Admin Abuse War challenge: TechWiser dates Jandel's X post to August 15, 2025 and Beebom dates it to August 16, 2025, which may be a time zone gap.
